During autoregressive generation, a support-projected causal consistency objective constrains token-level grounding within the risk-defined support mask. Evaluated on $\text{M}^3\text{TAVR}$, a comprehensive 1,482-patient cohort, TAVR-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art. It achieves an AUROC of 0.896, boosts CIDEr to 0.936, and drastically reduces the hallucination rate to 8.1\%, thereby improving interpretability for evidence-based surgical AI.

</details>


### [216] [A Pipeline for Generating Longitudinal Synthetic Clinical Notes Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26879)
*William Poulett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于生成合成临床笔记的模块化流水线和数据集，包含70名合成患者、每名患者20-50份临床笔记，旨在支持临床AI工具开发的同时避免真实患者数据的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于临床文档的敏感性，真实患者数据在AI开发中受到严格限制。为在保护隐私的前提下支持临床AI工具（如摘要、编码、决策支持系统）的开发与评估，需要一种能生成真实、多样且内部一致的合成临床数据的方法。

Method: 采用模块化流水线，结合结构化患者生成、半结构化患者历程模拟以及基于大语言模型的非结构化临床笔记生成。通过LLM验证和增强步骤提升忠实度、真实性和多样性，并确保纵向患者记录的内部一致性。数据集按多个验证级别提供，以平衡真实性与可扩展性。

Result: 生成了包含70名合成患者的数据集，每名患者关联20-50份覆盖完整住院历程的临床笔记。数据集提供多级验证选项，支持用户根据用例在真实性和可扩展性之间权衡。

Conclusion: 该合成临床笔记数据集可支持临床AI系统的开发、测试和评估，无需依赖真实患者数据，有效解决了数据隐私与AI工具开发之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used to enable the development and evaluation of AI systems in domains where access to real-world data is restricted. In healthcare, clinical documentation presents particular challenges due to its sensitivity. This work introduces a synthetic clinical notes pipeline and dataset designed to support the development of clinical AI tools while avoiding the privacy risks associated with real patient data. The dataset is generated using a modular pipeline that combines structured patient generation, semi-structured patient journey simulation, and unstructured clinical note generation using large language models. The pipeline is designed to prioritise internal consistency across longitudinal patient records, while also capturing variation in writing style, note structure, and clinical detail. Additional mechanisms, including LLM-based validation and augmentation steps, are used to improve faithfulness, realism, and diversity of the generated notes. We release a dataset of 70 synthetic patients, each associated with 20-50 clinical notes spanning a full hospital journey. The dataset is provided at multiple levels of validation, enabling users to balance realism and scalability depending on their use case. This dataset supports the development, testing, and evaluation of clinical AI systems, including summarisation tools, coding models, and decision support systems, without reliance on real patient data.

</details>


### [217] [Generative Retrieval via Diffusion Transformer with Metric-Ordered Sequence Training and Hybrid-Policy Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26899)
*Chenghao Liu,Yu Zhang,Zhongtao Jiang,Kun Xu,Zhenwei An,Renzhi Wang,Zhao Wang,Jiachen Zhang,Yuxiao Zhang,Kun Xu,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MO-DiT+HPPO框架，通过度量有序训练和混合偏好优化，解决模式保持属性检索中提升属性指标同时不偏离原模式的核心矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于嵌入的检索中，返回最高分项并非总是生产场景所需。给定表达细粒度模式的种子集，需要既满足目标属性又保持原模式的更多项，但这两个目标相互冲突：平均种子保持模式但处于低属性区域，全局属性检索会漂移到无关模式。

Method: 提出MO-DiT+HPPO分段框架：原始序列预训练、多领域度量有序连续预训练（将稀疏在线检索标签转化为从低到高预测属性密度的模式内轨迹，跨域统一学习度量改进方向）、尾部质心微调、HPPO（混合候选池用在线交集度量标注，参考锚定偏好优化，帕累托对过滤器仅保留不降低同模式纯度的胜出对）。

Result: 在四个属性域、项留存和模式留存协议下，度量有序DiT相较预训练生成式检索器提升交集度量，HPPO进一步提升，在八个域-分割实验单元中七个显著提升，最难分割上边际持平。

Conclusion: 度量有序训练和混合偏好优化能有效兼顾属性指标提升与模式保持，消融实验验证了各组件贡献来源。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval ranks items by their similarity to a query in a shared vector space and usually aims to return the highest-scoring items. In many production settings this is not what is wanted: given a seed set that expresses a fine-grained pattern, one needs more items that both satisfy a target attribute and stay within that pattern. We formalize this as pattern-preserving attribute retrieval. The two goals pull against each other: averaging the seeds preserves the pattern but stays in a low-attribute region, while global attribute retrieval drifts to unrelated patterns. We approach the task with continuous generative retrieval, where a model reads a sequence of item embeddings and generates query embeddings for nearest-neighbor search. We propose MO-DiT+HPPO, a staged framework with raw-sequence pretraining, multi-domain metric-ordered continuation pretraining, tail-centroid fine-tuning, and HPPO. Metric-ordered training turns sparse online retrieval labels into in-pattern trajectories ordered from low to high predicted attribute density, teaching one model the metric-improvement direction across domains. HPPO aligns the generated query distribution with the true online objective by labeling a hybrid candidate pool with the online intersection metric and applying reference-anchored preference optimization. A Pareto pair filter keeps only winner pairs that do not lower same-pattern purity, raising the attribute metric without sacrificing the pattern. Across four attribute domains under item- and pattern-holdout protocols, metric-ordered DiT improves the intersection metric over a pretrained generative retriever, and HPPO improves it further, with significant gains on seven of eight domain-split cells and a marginal tie on the hardest split. Metric-predictor validation, order ablations, CPT/SFT comparisons, and a candidate-policy ablation show where the gains come from.

</details>


### [218] [Learning to Recover Task Experts from a Multi-Task Merged Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26902)
*Jinwook Jung,Taegyu Kim,Kumju Jo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReTeX框架，将多任务模型合并中的参数干扰建模为仿射变换（可近似为加性偏移），通过预测偏移量从单个合并检查点中恢复任务专家性能，并利用SVD子空间签名实现无路由器的任务识别，在视觉和NLP领域恢复超过95%的个体专家性能，同时显著提升对未见任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务模型合并中静态合并存在参数干扰问题，而动态合并推理时需要存储和加载冗余专家组件，成本高昂。现有方法难以在不增加推理开销的前提下有效消除参数干扰。

Method: 1) 将参数干扰建模为仿射变换，近似为加性偏移；2) 提出ReTeX框架，通过预测参数偏移量来撤销干扰、恢复专家性能；3) 引入基于SVD子空间签名的无路由器任务标识器，推理前离线计算，推理时选择投影残差最小的子空间对应任务。

Result: 在视觉和NLP领域恢复超过95%的个体专家性能，显著提升对未见任务的泛化能力，并能自适应地插值已知专家知识处理分布外（OOD）任务。

Conclusion: 通过建模参数干扰为可预测的偏移量，ReTeX在无需额外专家组件存储和加载的情况下，实现了高效的多任务模型合并与任务识别，同时展现出对未知任务的适应性知识插值能力，为多任务学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging aims to consolidate several task-specific experts into a unified model, yet static merging consistently suffers from parameter interference. While dynamic merging models aim to bridge this gap, many works rely on the costly storage and loading of redundant expert components at inference. In this work, from the perspective of task expert, we view parameter interference as parameter perturbation introduced to each expert during merging process. We show that such parameter perturbations can be modeled as affine transformation, which can be approximated as additive offsets. Motivated by these, we propose Recover Task eXpert (ReTeX), a framework that predicts those offsets, in order to undo parameter interference and recover task-expert performance from a single merged checkpoint. To recover the appropriate expert when task identity is unknown, we introduce a router-free task identifier based on SVD subspace signatures computed offline before inference. At inference, the identifier selects the task whose subspace yields the smallest projection residual for a given input. As a result, ReTeX recovers over 95% of individual-expert performance in both vision and NLP domains, while significantly improving generalization to unseen tasks. Crucially, we also show that the parameter offset prediction leads to emergent adaptive interpolation of expert knowledge for out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. ReTeX adaptively interpolates seen expert knowledge to handle unseen tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/BAIKLAB/ReTeX

</details>


### [219] [Diagnosing Task Insensitivity in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26918)
*Jingyu Liu,Xiaopeng Wu,Kehan Chen,Chuan Yu,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型在长程代理任务中表现出色，但在分布外泛化方面存在弱点，原因是任务不敏感——模型倾向于应用训练时学到的模式而非遵循当前任务指令。本文提出任务扰动负对数似然优化（Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization）这一轻量对比正则化方法，通过显式鼓励动作对任务指令的依赖性，提升了任务敏感性和分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为长程代理时分布外泛化能力弱，关键原因是任务不敏感：模型在面对相似但不同的任务时，会套用训练时学到的模式而无法正确解决当前任务。

Method: 提出Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization（任务扰动负对数似然优化），一种轻量级的对比正则化方法，通过显式鼓励动作对任务指令的依赖性来提升任务敏感性。

Result: 实验表明该方法提升了任务敏感性和分布外泛化能力，同时保持了更稳定的对任务令牌的注意力分配。

Conclusion: 任务不敏感是影响LLM代理分布外泛化的重要因素，所提对比正则化方法能有效缓解该问题，使模型更稳定地关注任务指令。

Abstract: Large language models can serve as capable long-horizon agents, but their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains weak. We identify a key source of this failure as task insensitivity: when faced with similar but distinct tasks, models might apply patterns learned during training and fail to solve the task at hand. We show that models often continue with actions aligned with the original task even when the instruction is semantically corrupted and cannot be directly answered. We further find that, when we replace the task description in a trained prompt with another similar but distinct task, the model may still output the same action. This behavior is accompanied by a consistent training-time attention drift away from task tokens and toward local observations, suggesting an optimization bias toward shortcuts. To mitigate this problem, we propose Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization, a lightweight contrastive regularizer that explicitly encourages action dependence on the task instruction. Extensive evaluations show that our intervention improves task sensitivity and OOD generalization while preserving more stable attention to task tokens.

</details>


### [220] [Where Do CoT Training Gains Land in LLM based Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26935)
*Jingyu Liu,Zhiwen Wang,Yuxin Jing,Huanyu Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究链式推理(CoT)训练究竟提升了什么：是让模型更好地通过推理改变行动，还是让模型更擅长直接从提示中预测行动。研究发现CoT训练主要提升了直接预测行动（prompt action）的质量，而非CoT推理相对于直接预测的优势；后期检查点更少根据CoT修正行动；选择性屏蔽部分训练样本的动作令牌监督可提升跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明语言模型中的链式推理可能不是忠实的，而是一种事后的合理化解释（post-hoc reasoning），即模型在推理前就已知道答案。因此需要探究CoT训练到底改进了什么：是增强了通过推理改变行动的能力，还是增强了直接从提示预测行动的能力。

Method: 通过对比无CoT的prompt action（直接从提示预测行动）与有CoT的CoT action（通过推理预测行动）的质量，跨检查点评估；分析在环境交互中CoT行动相对于prompt行动的优势变化；检查后期检查点根据CoT修正行动的程度；并设计选择性屏蔽部分训练样本动作令牌监督的干预实验。

Result: 1) 跨检查点来看，prompt action质量显著提升；2) 环境交互中CoT行动相对prompt行动的优势保持稳定，说明CoT训练并未扩大CoT推理的优势，而是提升了prompt action的质量；3) 后期检查点更少根据CoT修正行动，更依赖直接提示；4) 选择性屏蔽动作令牌监督可提升跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoT训练主要提升的是模型直接从提示预测行动的能力，而非通过推理改变行动的能力；模型在训练过程中逐渐减少对CoT推理的依赖，更倾向于直接从提示中获取答案；通过适当减少对动作令牌的监督可以改善模型的泛化性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely used in language-model agents, but prior work has shown that verbalized CoT is not always faithful and may instead reflect post-hoc reasoning, which means the model already knows the answer before reasoning. We therefore ask what CoT training is actually improving: is the model getting better at changing its action through generated reasoning, or is it getting better at predicting the action directly from the prompt? We study this question by comparing \emph{prompt actions} (predicting action without CoT) with CoT actions (predicting action with CoT). Across checkpoints, prompt-action quality improves substantially. While interacting with the environment, the relative advantage of CoT actions over prompt actions remains similar, showing that CoT training does not widen the advantage of CoT reasoning, and it helps to improve the quality of prompt actions. We further find that later checkpoints are less likely to revise the action in response to CoT, suggesting greater reliance on the prompt. Motivated by these patterns, we selectively mask action-token supervision on a fraction of training examples. This intervention improves out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [221] [Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26964)
*Jiaming Bian,Bingliang Li,Yuehao Wu,Pichao Wang,Zhi Wang,Hailan Ma,Huadong Mo,Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Look-Before-Move框架，将动态3D故事世界中的相机规划问题形式化为叙事驱动的视觉注意力机制，通过先确定观察内容再执行运动的方式，显著提升了主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和世界模型需在动态3D环境中主动决定观察什么，而非被动处理观测。现有工作缺乏将叙事意图与物理3D约束结合、先决定观察再执行运动的系统性方法。

Method: 提出Look-Before-Move框架，包含三步：1) 语义观察合约（将导演意图转化为可执行的视觉约束）；2) 蒙特卡洛视点搜索（寻找符合叙事且几何可行的视点）；3) 语义轨迹接地（将选定视点连接为连续、无碰撞、时间连贯的相机运动）。

Result: 在基于StoryBlender构建的动态3D故事世界基准（50个故事、457个场景、1585个镜头）上，该方法在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量方面均优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 在生成相机运动之前先组织视觉注意力是提升动态3D场景中相机规划质量的关键步骤，验证了"先看再动"策略的有效性。

Abstract: As embodied AI and world models increasingly operate in dynamic 3D environments, visual perception must move beyond passively interpreting given observations toward actively deciding what to observe. We study this problem through camera planning in dynamic 3D story worlds, where the camera must not only generate smooth motion, but also decide what visual evidence should be acquired before it moves. We formulate this capability as Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention, where the camera acts as an embodied observer that determines what to observe, how to compose the observation, and how to shift attention over time under narrative intent and physical 3D constraints. To realize this capability, we propose Look-Before-Move, a camera planning framework that separates observation specification from motion execution. It first builds a Semantic Observation Contract to convert directorial intent into executable visual constraints, then performs Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search to find narrative-compliant and geometrically feasible viewpoints, and finally applies Semantic Trajectory Grounding to connect selected viewpoints into continuous, collision-aware, and temporally coherent camera motion. We further construct a dynamic 3D Story World Benchmark based on StoryBlender, covering 50 stories, 457 scenes, and 1585 shots with animated characters, semantic scene configurations, and executable 3D environments. Experiments show that our framework improves subject perception, intent consistency, and trajectory quality over representative baselines, demonstrating the importance of organizing visual attention before generating camera motion.

</details>


### [222] [Einstein World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26969)
*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Zangir Iklassov,Ali Mekky,Zayd M. Kawakibi Zuhri,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出爱因斯坦世界模型（EWM），一种让LLM通过调用世界模块生成视觉-时间推演作为推理轨迹的方法，使大语言模型能够利用视觉化反事实推理来增强复杂思考能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型是否可以通过视觉化反事实事件来补充纯语言推理的不足，从而处理超越直接经验的复杂思维。

Method: 提出爱因斯坦世界模型（EWM），在LLM推理轨迹中插入视觉-时间推演（世界模块生成的短场景推演），将其作为可检验的假设而非最终答案，辅助后续推理。这是将工具调用能力扩展到视觉思维实验领域。

Result: EWM使LLM能够利用视觉化反事实推演来支持推理，将视觉思维实验纳入语言模型的推理流程。

Conclusion: 视觉化反事实事件可以作为纯语言推理的补充机制，EWM为LLM提供了一种超越纯文本推理的复杂思维框架。

Abstract: Does intelligence require the ability to reason about phenomena beyond direct experience? It is natural to suspect that some complex thought cannot be captured through language alone. However, of particular concern to this work, is whether visualising counterfactual events can complement language as a mechanism for complex thought. We ask whether LLMs can be trained to utilise such visualisation mechanisms, in a way that benefits their reasoning abilities. Motivated by this question, we propose Einstein World Models. EWMs are a blueprint for LLM-based reasoning systems that place visual-temporal rollouts inside the reasoning trace, allowing them to reason in ways that text alone may not support well. In an EWM, the LLM calls a world-module (not to be confused with a world model), to produce short rollouts of scenes under consideration. The returned rollout is treated not as the answer, but as an inspectable hypothesis that can support later reasoning. Einstein World Models extend the capability of LLMs for tool calling (such as web search or code execution), into the domain of visual thought experiments.

</details>


### [223] [Adaptive Utility driven Resource Orchestration for Resilient AI (AURORA-AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27005)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Ilias Cherkaoui,Lizy Abraham,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AURORA-AI框架，通过Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman反馈控制、Lyapunov稳定性监控和公平感知复合效用的统一闭环策略，在非平稳条件下动态分配异构AI模型的计算资源，相比五种基线方法在恢复速度、公平性、稳定性和多目标效用上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统在非平稳的计算、人口统计和运行条件下部署时，静态资源分配策略会同时降低预测性能和公平性、可解释性等人本属性，亟需一种能够自适应应对干扰的弹性资源编排框架。

Method: 提出AURORA-AI框架，将Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman反馈控制、Lyapunov稳定性监控和公平感知复合效用函数统一为闭环策略，在异构AI模型群体上持续重分配计算预算，以最大化联合涵盖预测性能、人口统计均等、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性的全局效用。

Result: 在同时注入人口统计偏差冲击、渐进概念漂移和突发黑天鹅事件的应力仿真中，AURORA-AI实现即时恢复（Static基线88步、PPO 22步），将alpha分位数和super分位数分别提升29%和25%，同时降低均值和最大人口统计均等差距，并增加Lyapunov稳定运行步数占比。

Conclusion: 基于稳定性理论的公平感知自适应编排是实现弹性人本AI部署的一条实用且具有理论依据的途径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly deployed under non-stationary computational, demographic, and operational conditions in which static resource allocation strategies degrade both predictive performance and human-centric properties such as fairness and explainability. This paper presents AURORA-AI, an Adaptive Utility-driven Resource Orchestration framework for Resilient AI that unifies Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman feedback control, Lyapunov-based stability monitoring, and a fairness-aware composite utility into a single closed-loop policy.The framework continuously redistributes computational budget across a population of heterogeneous AI models so that the global utility, defined jointly over predictive performance, demographic parity, cost, latency, robustness, and interpretability, remains maximised under disruption. The framework is evaluated in a stress-rich discrete-time simulation that concurrently injects demographic bias shocks, gradual concept drift, and abrupt black-swan disruptions, and is compared against five established controllers including Static, Round Robin, Greedy, LinUCB, and a deep reinforcement-learning agent based on Proximal Policy Optimisation. AURORA-AI achieves immediate recovery from the black-swan event compared to eighty-eight time steps for the Static baseline and twenty-two for Proximal Policy Optimisation, lifts the alpha-quantile and the super-quantile by twenty-nine and twenty-five percent respectively, simultaneously reduces the mean and maximum demographic parity gap, and increases the fraction of Lyapunov-stable operating steps. These results indicate that fairness-aware adaptive orchestration grounded in stability theory is a practical and theoretically motivated path toward resilient human-centric AI deployment.

</details>


### [224] [Semantic Early-Stopping for Iterative LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27009)
*Sahil Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体LLM循环中的语义早停机制，通过监控连续草稿嵌入的余弦距离变化和答案质量改进来判断何时停止，替代了传统的固定迭代次数。理论层面证明了确定性终止和良定义性；提出了裁判高效评估协议；实验表明无裁判的语义停止器在HotpotQA上相比固定迭代次数节省38%的操作token且质量持平，而完整质量门控变体因裁判成本过高适得其反。Oracle分析将问题从"何时停止"重新定义为"哪一轮最佳"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM循环（如Writer-Critic架构）使用固定最大迭代次数作为终止条件，这种语法层面的"终止开关"无法感知答案是否仍在改进，导致简单输入浪费token而困难输入过早截断。亟需一种语义层面的早停机制来解决这一效率与质量的不平衡问题。

Method: 提出语义早停机制：当连续草稿嵌入的余弦距离在耐心窗口内停止变化且答案测量质量停止改进时终止循环。理论方面通过机器验证证明确定性终止和良定义性，将距离序列收敛性作为经验可检验的猜想而非过度宣称的Banach压缩。评估方面设计裁判高效协议：预先生成完整轨迹，回放所有停止策略于相同草稿，缓存LLM裁判调用，实现严格配对的效率-质量对比，并分离操作token与评估token。

Result: 在HotpotQA的60题测试集上，无裁判的语义停止器相比固定迭代次数节省38%操作token，质量持平（Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81）。完整质量门控变体因每轮裁判成本过高反而适得其反。Oracle选择最佳轮次相比所有实际策略高出+0.115信息分数（p ~ 4e-11）。

Conclusion: 语义早停在无裁判条件下能有效减少冗余计算且不损失质量；引入质量门控因裁判成本过高不可行。Oracle分析揭示了问题本质：关键不在于"何时停止"（已解决），而在于"哪一轮最佳"（仍为开放问题）。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) loops, for example a Writer that drafts and a Critic that revises, are almost always terminated by a fixed iteration cap (max_iterations). This is a syntactic kill-switch: it is blind to whether the answer is still improving, so it over-spends tokens on easy inputs and truncates hard ones. We study semantic early-stopping: the loop halts when consecutive draft embeddings stop changing in meaning (cosine distance with a patience window) and the answer's measured quality stops improving. Our work makes three contributions. First, an honest theoretical footing: we prove deterministic termination and well-definedness and machine-check these claims, while treating the convergence of the distance sequence as an empirically tested conjecture rather than a (previously over-claimed) Banach contraction. Second, a judge-efficient evaluation protocol: we generate each question's full trajectory once, replay every stopping policy over the identical drafts, and cache every LLM-judge call, yielding a strictly paired efficiency-versus-quality comparison at low cost; we further separate operational tokens (charged to a policy) from evaluation tokens (a measurement instrument). Third, an empirical study on multi-hop retrieval-augmented question answering (HotpotQA). On the 60-question test split, a judge-free semantic stopper reduces operational tokens by 38% relative to max_iterations at parity quality (Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81), whereas the full quality-gated variant is counter-productive because its per-round judging dominates cost. An oracle that selects the best round attains +0.115 Information Score over every practical policy (p ~ 4e-11), reframing the problem from "when to stop" (easy) to "which round is best" (open).

</details>


### [225] [How to evaluate clustering with ground truth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27061)
*Pasi Fränti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于集合匹配的外部聚类有效性指标，推荐质心指数（CI）作为直观的聚类级度量，并讨论了点级度量（如PSI和ACC）的适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当有真实标签可用时，需要有效的外部指标来评估聚类质量。本文旨在梳理常见的基于集合匹配的外部有效性指标，并为不同应用场景推荐合适的度量方法。

Method: 回顾了基于集合匹配的外部聚类有效性指标，重点比较了质心指数（CI）、对集指数（PSI）和聚类准确率（ACC）等度量方法的特点和适用场景。

Result: 质心指数（CI）是直观且可解释的聚类级度量；对集指数（PSI）提供归一化分数且不受聚类大小偏差影响；聚类准确率（ACC）及其它集合匹配度量适用于所有点权重相等的场景。

Conclusion: 推荐CI作为聚类级度量的首选；若需要更精细的点级度量，PSI和ACC各有其适用优势，应根据具体需求选择。

Abstract: External indexes can be used for cluster evaluation when ground truth is available. We review the most common external validity indexes focusing on set-matching-based measures. We recommend centroid index (CI), because it is an intuitive cluster-level measure with an explainable result. If we need a more fine-tuned, point-level measure, there are more choices. Pair-set index (PSI) provides a normalized score which is not biased by cluster sizes. If all points should matter equally, then clustering accuracy (ACC) or any other set-matching measure is suitable.

</details>


### [226] [Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27136)
*Shicheng Ye,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JERP提出了一种联合学习方法，在同一交互轨迹上同时更新长期经验规则池和LLM智能体策略，使规则与演化策略保持对齐，同时在稀疏奖励环境中提供局部修正。在AlfWorld和WebShop上取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将交互经验分为两类使用：作为自然语言规则保存在模型外供后续提示（易解释但易与演化策略脱节），或使用轨迹和反馈更新模型参数（策略提升更广泛但在稀疏奖励场景下对局部错误修正有限）。需要一种既能保持规则与策略对齐又能同时利用两种经验优势的方法。

Method: JERP维护一个长期经验规则池，决策时检索任务相关规则并连同交互历史一起作为条件输入智能体。每个回合结束后，利用收集的轨迹同时优化策略和修订规则池（通过对比当前 rollout 与参考成功轨迹）。

Result: 在AlfWorld和WebShop两个复杂交互任务基准上，JERP相比基线方法取得了一致性的决策性能提升。

Conclusion: 规则池与策略的联合更新能使规则与演化策略保持对齐，同时稳定有效的行为可逐渐被吸收到模型本身中，在复杂交互任务上优于分离使用经验的现有方法。

Abstract: For LLM agents in multi-step interactive environments, a key challenge is to make effective use of accumulated interaction experience. Existing work has typically separated two uses of such experience: keeping it outside the model as natural-language rules for later prompting, or using trajectories and feedback to update the model parameters. The former is easy to interpret but can fall out of sync with the evolving policy; the latter improves the policy more broadly but provides only limited correction for local mistakes in sparse-reward settings. We present Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents (JERP), which updates a long-term experiential-rule pool and the policy from the same interaction trajectories. At decision time, JERP retrieves task-relevant rules and conditions the agent on them together with the interaction history. After each episode, it uses the collected trajectories both to optimize the policy and to revise the rule pool by comparing current rollouts with reference successful trajectories. This coupling keeps the rule pool aligned with the evolving policy while allowing stable and effective behaviors to be gradually absorbed into the model itself. Experiments on AlfWorld and WebShop show that JERP yields consistent gains in decision performance for complex interactive tasks.

</details>


### [227] [OpenRCA 2.0: From Outcome Labels to Causal Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27154)
*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAVE是一个逐步标注协议，用于构建首个跨系统RCA基准OpenRCA 2.0，揭示了LLM智能体在根因分析中因果传播路径定位的严重缺陷（仅61.5%能正确链接因果），而传统仅标注根因的评估方式掩盖了这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA数据集仅标注根因而不标注从根因到症状的因果传播路径，导致任务退化为简单的模式匹配，无法真正评估LLM智能体的多步推理和因果理解能力。

Method: 提出PAVE逐步标注协议，利用故障注入中的已知干预措施，采用前向验证机制（从原因到结果正向推理）重建因果传播路径，并构建了包含500个实例的OpenRCA 2.0基准。

Result: 在11个前沿LLM上，精确恢复完整根因集平均仅20.7%；76.0%能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将根因服务通过已验证的因果传播路径连接到观测症状，即存在"未接地诊断"问题。

Conclusion: 仅评估根因结果（outcome-only）会隐藏因果路径定位失败这一重要缺陷，逐步因果标注是实现可信LLM-based RCA智能体的关键缺失环节。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [228] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang,Yichen Guo,Rui Huang,Zheng Lu,Qizhe Zhang,Chenxi Li,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Zhirong Shen,Yaosong Du,Guangyan Gan,Wenya Wang,Lin William Cong,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，将视觉标记剪枝形式化为构建最优保留集（Token Optimal Preservation Sets, TOPS），通过信息论分析提出任务相关性、信息覆盖和语义多样性三大原则，设计出无需训练、模型无关的剪枝模块，在7个MLLM骨干和14个基准上超越现有方法，在LLaVA-NeXT上剪除77.8%视觉标记仍保持甚至提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽具备强大推理能力，但大量视觉标记导致计算开销巨大；现有视觉标记剪枝方法存在不足：基于注意力标准会保留冗余标记，基于多样性标准又忽视用户指令，即使结合多标准也缺乏对剪枝内在目标的原理性描述。

Method: 从第一性原理重新审视视觉标记剪枝，通过自上而下的信息论分析，识别出任务相关性（Task Relevance）、信息覆盖（Information Coverage）和语义多样性（Semantic Diversity）三个基本原则；基于这些原则提出TOPS——一个无需训练、模型无关的剪枝模块，可应用于多种MLLM。

Result: 在7个MLLM骨干和14个基准上的广泛实验表明，TOPS在各种剪枝设置下均优于先前方法；在LLaVA-NeXT上，TOPS剪除77.8%的视觉标记，在7B和13B模型上分别保持100.0%和100.6%的性能。

Conclusion: 剪除冗余视觉标记有时可以减轻幻觉问题，并启发未来轻量级MLLM的设计。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>


### [229] [A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27188)
*Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出过程护栏（Process Harness）机制，通过在确定性工作流引擎外围部署策略管控的智能体层，在不替换底层引擎的前提下将传统工作流升级为智能体业务流程管理（Agentic BPM）。基于任务-决策-流（TDF）模型及其实现CUGA FLO，在贷款审批流程中验证了三种策略管控智能体类型和钩子驱动的监管覆写机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工作流引擎虽能保证结构化合规性，但缺乏应对复杂、动态场景的推理与适应能力；完全替换为LLM驱动的智能体系统又面临可靠性、安全性和合规性风险。需要一种折中方案，在不破坏原有确定性工作流结构的前提下引入智能体推理与自适应能力。

Method: 1. 提出过程护栏（Process Harness）机制：在确定性工作流引擎外围部署策略管控的智能体层；2. 定义任务-决策-流（TDF）模型，包含数据模式和执行语义；3. 设计三种策略管控智能体：TaskAgent（知识密集型任务执行）、DecisionAgent（逐例网关路由）、FlowAgent（基于钩子机制运行时流适配）；4. 提出过程FRAME策略集统一管控所有LLM调用；5. 实现CUGA FLO系统并在贷款审批流程中验证。

Result: 在贷款审批工作流上成功实现了三种智能体类型和钩子驱动的监管覆写机制，证明了过程护栏能够同时满足命令性需求（通过确定性工作流执行保证结构合规性）和规范性需求（通过策略框架下的智能体自主性在指定控制点进行推理、适应与监督）。

Conclusion: 过程护栏机制有效调和了确定性执行与智能体自主性之间的矛盾，为传统工作流向Agentic BPM演进提供了一条可行的增量升级路径。

Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [230] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAversary，一种基于遗传算法的黑盒对抗攻击方法，利用GloVe嵌入进行词替换（突变算子），在多个基准数据集上显著降低目标模型准确率（最佳情况从76.8%降至5.8%），但扰动词数约为对比方法的2倍，语义相似度略低，运行时间增加约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在NLP领域易受对抗攻击，现有攻击方法需要访问模型内部结构或生成的对抗样本语义相似度不高，亟需一种既能保持语义相似性又只需黑盒访问的高效攻击方法。

Method: 提出GAversary，一种混合遗传算法（GA），将目标模型视为黑盒，仅利用模型输出的logit值指导搜索；使用GloVe词嵌入作为突变算子来生成语义相似的词替换，从而提升对抗样本的语义相似性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和知名目标模型上，GAversary相比BAE和A2T攻击方法，在最佳情况下将目标模型准确率从76.8%降至5.8%（BAE降至27.6%）；但扰动词数约为对比方法的2倍，语义相似度略低，运行时间增加约5%。

Conclusion: GAversary以黑盒方式实现了更强的对抗攻击效果，虽在扰动词数、语义相似度和运行时间上有所折衷，但攻击有效性显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [231] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL 是一个将评估标准分解为原子二元问题，并由 LLM 独立回答以生成可解释、多维评分的框架。在 SummEval、Topical-Chat 和 QAGS 上，它匹配或超越了 UniEval、G-Eval 等强基线，尤其在事实一致性基准上表现优异，且能避免天花板效应，支持基于问题级反馈的迭代提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 输出评估是 NLP 的瓶颈：人工评估昂贵且缓慢，词汇级指标与开放生成的人类判断相关性差，整体式 LLM 评判产生不透明且难以调试的分数。

Method: 将评估标准分解为原子二元问题，通过元提示生成细粒度评估问题，让 LLM 独立回答每个问题，汇总出校准的多维分数和可解释的问题级反馈。

Result: 在 SummEval、Topical-Chat 和 QAGS 上匹配或超越 UniEval、G-Eval 等强基线，在 QAGS 等事实一致性基准上尤为突出；更好匹配人类分数分布，避免天花板效应；支持迭代提示优化，在自更新和跨模型更新设置下均有提升。

Conclusion: BINEVAL 是一个任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，兼具强实证性能与实用的诊断和优化价值。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [232] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EO-WM，一种用于多光谱地球观测预测的视频扩散Transformer，通过物理感知的条件框架（气候基线、天气异常和累积物理应力信号）来捕捉天气驱动的地表动态变化，并引入两个诊断基准（极端夏季基准和季节匹配对基准）来评估模型对天气变化的响应能力，在NDVI下降幅度误差和方向命中率上分别相对提升5.63%和7.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将地球观测预测视为确定性回归问题导致不确定性坍缩，或扩散模型将天气变量作为无差别的条件信号，缺乏对天气-地表物理耦合过程的显式建模；同时现有基准侧重重建精度而非评估模型对天气变化的正确响应。

Method: 提出EO-WM视频扩散Transformer，包含物理感知的条件框架：将气象强迫分解为气候基线、天气异常和累积物理应力信号（累积异常强迫以捕捉持续热/干旱胁迫），通过不同的条件路径分别处理基线和异常。

Result: 预测NDVI下降幅度误差相对降低5.63%，方向命中率相对提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过物理感知的条件建模和专门的气候响应基准，EO-WM在天气驱动的植被动态预测上优于现有方法，证明了分离气候基线/异常和累积应力信号的有效性。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [233] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于神经后验估计的模拟推断（SBI）作为MCMC的可扩展替代方案，用于贝叶斯校准机械流行病学模型。以德国2020年COVID-19的ICU占用数据为例，在31天和201天两种推断窗口下与MCMC进行对比，结果显示SBI能恢复与MCMC高度一致的后验分布，同时显著降低计算时间（从MCMC的数千秒减少到SBI的数十至数百秒），为近实时疫情分析和重复推断提供了高效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCMC在高维非线性系统和重复近实时分析中存在计算成本高的问题，需要一种更快速的可扩展贝叶斯校准方法以支持传染病预测和公共卫生决策。

Method: 采用基于神经后验估计的模拟推断（SBI）作为替代方案，结合CPU和GPU资源；以SECIR机械流行病学模型为研究对象，利用德国2020年COVID-19的ICU占用数据；分别在31天推断窗口和201天多传播变化点重建问题下，使用Wasserstein距离、KL散度和后验预测检查定量评估SBI与MCMC的后验一致性。

Result: 在31天窗口中，SBI恢复的后验分布与MCMC高度一致，且准确再现观察到的ICU轨迹；在201天设置下，SBI保留了主要后验结构，尽管不确定性增加。计算时间方面：MCMC在31天问题需约1000秒，SBI仅需60-70秒（单GPU）；201天问题MCMC需超19000秒，SBI平均仅需157秒。

Conclusion: SBI为机械流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准提供了一个快速且计算高效的框架，能够支持重复近实时推断和快速爆发分析。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [234] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在自动化简历筛选中使用提示注入（Prompt Injection）策略对LLM评估结果的影响。实验表明，当简历质量同质化且少量候选人使用该策略时，提示注入能有效提升排名；但随着使用人数增多，效果迅速减弱；在候选人质量异质时，该方法平均效果下降，但偶尔会让低质量候选人超越高质量候选人，引发公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛应用于简历筛选和候选人排名，候选人可能通过策略性手段操纵算法招聘系统。本研究旨在探究提示注入这种隐蔽的自我推销文本对LLM评估结果的影响，以及其在不同情境下的有效性和公平性影响。

Method: 通过控制实验，设计不同场景（候选人质量同质/异质、注入者比例变化），测试提示注入对LLM排名结果的影响。

Result: 提示注入在简历质量同质且少数候选人使用时效果显著；随着注入人数增多效果迅速下降；在质量异质时平均效果较低，但偶发情况下能让低质量候选人超越高质量候选人。

Conclusion: LLM筛选系统在操纵行为罕见且候选人质量差异小时最为脆弱。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [235] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模型LLM系统（路由、投票、级联等）的精度提升存在一个上限：无法超过1减去所有模型同时出错的概率β。传统诊断指标ρ（平均成对误差相关性）无法识别β。通过67个模型的实证分析发现，高斯copula模型严重低估了β值（约2.5倍），且在多选题改为自由回答形式后，β显著增大。在可检查任务中，组合模型很少能超越单一最佳模型，除非有强大的查询级路由信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛报道多模型LLM系统（路由、投票、级联、融合等）能提升单模型准确性，但忽略了其增益存在理论上限。作者旨在揭示这一上限（由所有模型同时出错的概率β决定），并指出常用诊断指标ρ无法识别β，从而纠正领域内的认知偏差。

Method: 理论推导：证明任何输出为某个模型答案的策略，其准确率不超过1-β（所有模型同时出错的概率）。对比ρ（平均成对误差相关性）与β的区别，指出相同边际分布和ρ下β可能不同。使用Clopper-Pearson界给出β的有限样本置信度。实证：收集21个提供商的67个模型，在开放数学、执行代码、GPQA-Diamond（自由回答形式）等任务上计算β，并与tetrachoric校准的单因子模型（高斯copula）进行比较。

Result: 在开放数学任务上，观测β=0.052，而67模型高斯copula预测β=0.023，低估约2.5倍（90% CI: 1.7-3.4, k=17）。在代码任务上，β=0.079。GPQA-Diamond改为自由回答后β=0.127，五评委小组kappa 0.73-0.92，表明共失败源于答案格式而非学科。低ρ异质集成在匹配质量下优于高ρ Self-MoA。但在可检查任务中，组合模型很少超越单一最佳模型，除非有强查询级路由信号。

Conclusion: 多模型系统的增益上限由所有模型同时出错的概率β决定，而非ρ。β被现有模型严重低估。真正的增益来源是模型在不同问题上失败（异质性），而非简单增加模型数量。在可检查任务中，缺乏强路由信号时，组合模型难以超越单一最佳模型。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [236] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言数字孪生的框架，利用大语言模型结合风格特征和上下文元数据模拟老年人对话行为，并通过多头条件变分自编码器评估重建质量与认知评分预测。实验表明该框架能保留个体身份特征，在重建和MoCA预测误差上与真实数据相当，优于GPT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期检测在认知健康领域仍具挑战性，而语言和对话模式可作为非侵入性生物标志物。现有方法缺乏个性化建模能力，亟需一种可扩展、非侵入式的持续认知健康监测方法。

Method: 提出基于语言的数字孪生框架：1）利用LLM结合风格特征和上下文元数据模拟老年人对话行为；2）引入多头条件变分自编码器（cVAE），联合衡量重建质量并预测认知评分（如MoCA）。

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上，该数字孪生保留了身份特定特征，重建误差和MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，且优于GPT生成的基线响应。

Conclusion: 基于语言的数字孪生作为一种可扩展、非侵入式方法，在个性化、持续性的认知健康监测方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Having Dog Ears "for Real": Effects of Active and Passive Haptics on Embodying Non-Human Body Parts in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26364)
*Omar A. Khan,Bao Han Trinh,Lee Lisle,Tiffany D. Do*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了VR中非人体部位的具身感，通过在线调查确定狗耳朵为常见研究对象，并开展2x2被试内实验比较主动触觉（振动手套）和被动触觉（物理头带）及其组合对具身感的影响。结果显示被动触觉效果最佳，组合模态会削弱被动触觉的优势，且非人体部位的具身感与整体化身具身感正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR中亚文化群体普遍存在非人体部位的化身实践，但真实与虚拟身体形态的差异会降低具身感。触觉反馈可补偿此差距，然而不同触觉模态及其组合对具身感的影响尚未被系统比较，存在文献空白。

Method: (1) 面向社交VR社区进行在线调查（n=63），确定动物耳朵为生态效度高的非人体部位研究对象；(2) 开展2×2被试内用户实验（n=28），自变量为主动触觉（振动手套）和被动触觉（物理头带），因变量为参与者触摸虚拟狗耳朵时的具身感测量。

Result: (1) 被动触觉产生了最强的整体具身感；(2) 组合模态反而降低了被动触觉的益处；(3) 非人体部位的具身感与整个化身的具身感呈正相关。

Conclusion: 被动触觉在增强非人体部位具身感方面优于主动触觉及两者组合，组合使用可能产生干扰效应；非人体部位的具身感可正向迁移至整体化身体验。研究结果对VR具身感文献及相关应用领域具有启示意义。

Abstract: Embodying non-human body parts in VR is a prevalent practice among certain subcultures and is a personally important creative outlet to many individuals. However, the discrepant morphology between real and virtual bodies can decrease Sense of Embodiment (SoE). Haptic feedback can compensate by increasing SoE felt towards non-human body parts, but there is a literature gap in comparing the effects of different haptic modalities, and their combinations, on SoE. Through an online survey sent out to social VR communities (n = 63), we determined that animal ears are a commonly embodied and ecologically valid non-human body part to study. We then ran a 2x2 within-subjects user study (n = 28) with two independent variables: active haptics, delivered through vibrotactile gloves, and passive haptics, delivered through a physical headband, for when participants reach up to touch virtual dog ears appended to their avatar in VR. Our findings show that (1) passive haptics produced the strongest overall embodiment outcomes, (2) combining modalities reduced the benefits of passive haptics, and (3) SoE towards non-human body parts positively correlates with SoE towards the entire avatar. We discuss implications of our findings in various domains, and on embodiment literature.

</details>


### [238] [Assistive Visual Cues for Visual Neglect Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26407)
*Per Bjerre,Andreas Køllund Pedersen,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了内源性和外源性线索对视觉忽视患者注意力引导的影响，发现内源性线索在患者忽视区域表现更好，两者结合可进一步改善，但个体差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往线索研究多针对正常视力人群，而视觉忽视患者需要有效的视觉线索来辅助治疗和引导注意力，因此有必要专门研究不同类型线索对此类患者的效果。

Method: 通过实验比较内源性线索（基于语义或任务目标的自上而下线索）和外源性线索（基于刺激突显性的自下而上线索）对视觉忽视患者在忽视区域内目标获取时间的影响，并分析两种线索结合的效果。

Result: 视觉忽视患者在内源性线索下表现更好，目标获取时间更短；部分情况下内外线索结合可进一步提升性能；最佳内源性线索平均获取时间3.5秒，最佳外源性为6.5秒，结合后降至2.8秒；但患者间差异很大。

Conclusion: 内源性线索对视觉忽视患者更有效，线索组合可能提供额外收益，但需考虑个体化差异进行针对性治疗设计。

Abstract: Previous research on exogenous and endogenous cues has shown how they direct attention and improve interaction speed and error rate in applications. However, most studies focus on people with normal sight. People suffering from visual neglect have difficulties attending to parts of the visual field. One treatment method calls for the use of strong visual cues to remind patients of their neglected area and help guide their attention to it. Therefore, we examine the effects of endogenous and exogenous cues on visual neglect patients. Our results showed that visual neglect patients perform better with endogenous cues, when targets are within their neglected area. In some cases, combining exogenous and endogenous cues improve performance further. However, the performance varies greatly between patients. Using one neglect patient as an example, we saw that the best endogenous cue had an average acquisition time of 3.5 seconds compared to 6.5 for the best exogenous. Combining exogenous and endogenous cues further improved acquisition time to 2.8 seconds.

</details>


### [239] [TinyCNNDeep: Lightweight Attention-Based CNN for EEG Classification of Eye States and Sleep Deprivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26506)
*Thien Nhan Vo,Yen Nhi Tran Thi,Ngan Nguyen Xuan Phuong,Xuan-The Tran*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出TinyCNNDeep轻量卷积网络，将多通道EEG转换为224×224灰度图像，结合残差学习和SE注意力模块，在正常睡眠与睡眠剥夺条件下四分类（睡眠条件×眼状态）任务中达到83.69%准确率，远超基线方法（47.66%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠剥夺损害警觉性和认知功能，但联合识别睡眠条件（正常/剥夺）和眼状态（睁/闭）在EEG研究中尚未被充分探索，现有方法难以同时建模这两个维度。

Method: 提出TinyCNNDeep：轻量CNN结合残差学习与Squeeze-and-Excitation注意力模块；将5通道（Fp1,Fp2,O1, Oz, O2）短EEG片段经Z-score归一化、min-max缩放和中心填充转换为224×224灰度图像，利用2D卷积联合建模通道间和时间结构。

Result: 在35名受试者数据集上，TinyCNNDeep达到83.69%留一受试者平均准确率，超越最强基线（随机森林，47.66%）36个百分点，而EEGNet、ShallowConvNet、DeepConvNet等现有架构接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 基于图像的EEG表示配合残差特征提取和通道注意力机制，能在最少电极配置下为多类睡眠相关EEG分类提供准确且计算高效的框架。

Abstract: Sleep deprivation impairs vigilance and cognitive function, yet jointly identifying the sleep condition (normal vs deprived) and the eye state (open vs closed) from electroencephalography (EEG) remains underexplored. We address this four-class problem with TinyCNNDeep, a lightweight convolutional neural network that combines residual learning with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) attention module. We convert short multi-channel EEG segments from five physiologically relevant channels (Fp1, Fp2, O1, Oz, O2) into 224x224 grayscale images through per-channel Z-score normalization, min-max scaling, and center padding, enabling 2D convolutions to jointly model inter-channel and temporal structure. On a 35-subject dataset recorded under normal-sleep and sleep-deprivation sessions, TinyCNNDeep attains a subject-wise mean accuracy of 83.69%, outperforming the strongest baseline (Random Forest with combined time-frequency features, 47.66%) by 36.03 percentage points, while three established EEG architectures (EEGNet, ShallowConvNet, DeepConvNet) operate near chance. Per-subject analysis quantifies inter-subject variability, and confusion-matrix inspection shows that residual misclassifications concentrate between eyes-closed states across sleep conditions. These results indicate that an image-based EEG representation paired with residual feature extraction and channel attention provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for multiclass sleep-related EEG classification under a minimal electrode configuration.

</details>


### [240] [DanceDuo: Bridging Human Movement and AI Choreography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26507)
*Gia-Cat Bui-Le,Tuong-Vy Truong-Thuy,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DanceDuo是一个基于扩散模型的AI舞蹈生成平台，支持音乐驱动的舞蹈合成、用户上传视频对比，并通过人体姿态估计提供表演分析。用户研究显示界面直观，对比功能备受好评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用深度学习和生成模型的进步，开发一个鼓励舞蹈练习的交互式AI平台，使用户能通过音乐选择、模型选择和自我视频对比等方式参与舞蹈创作与练习。

Method: 采用扩散模型生成与多种音乐风格同步的AI编舞序列；集成人体姿态估计模型，将用户的真实表演与AI生成序列进行对比分析。

Result: 通过用户研究发现，用户认为界面直观易用，尤其对舞蹈对比功能给予高度评价。

Conclusion: DanceDuo显著推动了AI在舞蹈编排领域的应用，为娱乐和专业用途提供了新的途径。

Abstract: In recent years, advancements in deep learning and generative models have revolutionized music-driven dance generation. This paper introduces a novel platform, namely DanceDuo, leveraging diffusion models to generate AI-choreographed dance sequences synchronized with a variety of music genres, to encourage dancing practice. The system allows users to interact with AI by selecting music tracks, humanoid models, and importing personal dance videos for comparison, fostering a rich and engaging user experience. DanceDuo not only offers dance generation but also integrates human pose estimation models to provide users with insightful comparisons of their own performances with AI-generated sequences. We conducted a comprehensive user study, revealing that users found the interface intuitive, with particular praise for the dance comparison feature. Our DanceDuo contributes significantly to the integration of AI in dance choreography, offering novel avenues for both recreational and professional applications.

</details>


### [241] [Budget-Aware Keyboardless Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26508)
*Quang-Thang Nguyen,Gia-Phuc Song-Dong,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用普通摄像头和打印键盘布局纸张的虚拟键盘系统，通过结合现代分割检测模型与传统图像处理算法识别键盘区域，并基于指甲颜色分析进行触键检测，无需复杂校准或特殊光照条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输入设备（键盘、鼠标、显示器）限制了用户的移动性，而现有虚拟键盘方案往往需要复杂设置或昂贵设备，亟需一种简便、低成本且能适应标准环境的虚拟键盘解决方案。

Method: 仅使用标准摄像头和打印键盘布局的纸张，结合现代分割与检测模型及传统图像处理算法识别键盘区域，通过分析用户指甲颜色进行触键检测。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的键盘和击键检测方案在实际应用中取得了有前景的效果，参与用户研究的受试者也认为该系统具有趣味性。

Conclusion: 该虚拟键盘系统无需复杂校准或特殊光照条件，在标准环境下即可工作，为移动场景下的文本输入提供了一种低成本、易部署的可行方案。

Abstract: Interacting with computers typically relies on traditional input devices such as keyboards, mice, and monitors, which can be cumbersome for users seeking greater mobility. Virtual keyboards have been explored to address these limitations, but they often involve complex setups or expensive equipment. This paper proposes a novel virtual keyboard system that leverages only a standard camera and a paper with a printed keyboard layout. Unlike previous methods requiring complex calibration or special lighting conditions, our approach can work on standard environment using modern computer vision technologies. Combining modern segmentation and detection models with traditional image processing algorithms, we efficiently identify the keyboard region. Touch detection is performed using an algorithm analyzing the color of the user's fingernail. Experiments demonstrated a promising results our proposed solution of keyboard and keystroke detection for practical applications. Participants attended our user study also found the proposed system interesting.

</details>


### [242] [Co-Designing Community-Centered AI Education for Adults: A Midwestern Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26565)
*Yao Lyu,Leonymae Aumentado,Holden Winton,Jared Lee Katzman,Sparkle Berry,Zachary Rowe,Kimberly Sanders,Tawanna R. Dillahunt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项针对非高等教育阶段成年人的AI教育案例研究，参与者为54名来自美国中西部城市东区非裔美国人社区的成年人。通过社区合作设计的教育 session，研究发现参与者对AI的担忧持续存在，但转向了更具体、基于本地情境的问题。论文从社区能力视角讨论AI素养，主张建立扎根于本地社区情境的AI素养框架以增强社区能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育日益重要，但高等教育体系之外的成年人群体受到的关注不足。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索非正式教育体系下成人AI教育的实践方式，并从社区层面揭示AI素养的现状与需求。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，与社区合作伙伴共同设计并实施了一场AI教育 session，参与者为54名成年人（48人线下、6人线上），主要来自非裔美国人社区。通过观察和互动收集数据，分析参与者对AI的态度变化及其提出的具体问题。

Result: 参与者对AI的担忧并未消失，但从泛化的焦虑转变为关于AI设计与部署的具体、本地化问题。这表明社区层面的AI教育能够帮助参与者更精准地识别和表达与自身生活相关的AI议题。

Conclusion: AI素养应从社区能力视角进行理解，而非仅关注个体知识水平。研究主张构建扎根于本地社区情境的AI素养框架，以增强社区在AI议题上的自主能力和应对能力。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) education is increasingly important, yet adults outside higher education receive less attention. We report a case study of an AI education session with 54 adults (48 in-person and 6 virtual) in a predominantly African American community on the east side of a major Midwestern city. We ask: "What does AI education for adults outside formal educational systems look like in practice?" and "What does this AI education session reveal about AI literacy at the community level?" Through a co-designed session developed with community partners, we found that concerns about AI persisted but shifted to specific, locally grounded questions about AI design and deployment. We also discuss AI literacy from a community capacity perspective and argue for AI literacy frameworks grounded in local community contexts that strengthen community capacity.

</details>


### [243] [An exploratory behavioral and electroencephalographic study of artificial intelligence-assisted learning modes in high school students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26579)
*Kashika Khurana,Ally Liew*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过48名青少年的EEG和行为数据，考察了三种AI交互模式（导师、协作者、解答者）对认知参与的影响，发现行为层面存在显著差异，EEG模式虽未达统计显著但值得进一步探究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将AI接触视为二元条件（有AI vs. 无AI），缺乏对交互模态及接触后效应的区分，亟需探究不同AI交互模式对认知参与和问题解决行为的差异化影响。

Method: 48名14-18岁青少年（男女各半）在两种平衡测验中采用被试内设计，记录EEG频谱活动及三种行为特征（启动、处理、压力）的量化指标，使用Friedman检验、重复测量方差分析、配对t检验和效应量进行统计分析。

Result: 三种AI交互模式在全部三项行为指标上均存在显著差异；EEG频谱模式在描述性层面呈现差异，但未达到统计显著性；初步观察到AI接触的短期遗留效应。

Conclusion: 研究提供了不同AI交互模式下神经活动和行为差异的初步行为证据与电生理观察，建立了可复现的人机交互研究框架，为进一步探究奠定了基础。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly integrating into education, concerns have emerged regarding its potential implications on cognitive engagement and problem-solving behavior. However, existing research largely treats AI exposure as a binary condition (AI vs. no-AI), with limited differentiation between interaction modalities and post-exposure effects. This study investigates whether distinct AI interaction modes (Tutor, Collaborator, Solver) influence frontal EEG spectral activity. Electroencephalography (EEG) data and quantified behavioral metrics were recorded from 48 study participants (24 males, 24 females; ages 14-18) across two counterbalanced quizzes in a within-subject design. Statistical analyses included Friedman tests, repeated-measures ANOVA, paired t-tests, and effect size calculations. Behavioral changes were mathematically analyzed in an observation matrix of three characteristics -Initiation, Processing, and Stress-measured on an ordinal scale. Each mode showed significant differences in all three behavioral measures. Descriptive EEG patterns in AI interaction mode were observed, and the possibility of short-term carryover effects of AI was explored. Although the EEG data did not reach statistical significance, the patterns observed across the three AI interaction modes warrant further investigation. This study provides preliminary behavioral evidence and investigative electrophysiological observations, exploring possible AI-interaction-mode-based differences in neural activity and behavior, while establishing a replicable framework for future human-AI interaction studies.

</details>


### [244] [HiLSVA: Design and Evaluation of a Human-in-the-Loop Agentic System for Scientific Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26614)
*Kuangshi Ai,Patrick Phuoc Do,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HiLSVA 是一个支持人机协同的科学可视化智能体系统，通过“先规划后执行”的多智能体架构、人类监督、逐步溯源追踪和测试时自适应学习，实现了混合主动交互。用户研究和案例分析表明，该方法在提升任务完成度、用户控制和工作流透明度的同时，揭示了执行效率与人类监督之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的科学可视化智能体系统过度强调自主性，缺乏对人类分析控制的重视，导致透明度和人类监督不足。作者旨在将智能体科学可视化重新定位为增强而非替代人类分析推理的协作过程。

Method: 提出 HiLSVA 系统，包含：(1) 先规划后执行的多智能体架构；(2) 明确的人类监督机制；(3) 逐步溯源追踪；(4) 测试时的用户反馈自适应学习。系统支持通过自然语言和直接操纵可视化实现人机流畅交接，并通过沙盒执行保证安全可复现的工作流。

Result: 通过代表性案例研究和12名不同专业水平参与者的受控用户研究表明，混合主动交互在不同用户专业水平下均能提升任务完成度、用户控制和工作流透明度，同时揭示了执行效率与人类监督之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 混主动交互的重要性在于以人为中心的设计，为未来协作式可视化系统的开发提供了指导方向。代码和演示视频已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents enable natural language interaction for scientific visualization (SciVis). Still, prior systems have essentially prioritized autonomy over human analytical control, thereby limiting transparency and human oversight. We present HiLSVA, a human-in-the-loop agentic system that supports mixed-initiative SciVis workflows. HiLSVA integrates a plan-first multi-agent architecture with explicit human oversight, stepwise provenance tracking, and learn-at-test-time adaptation from user feedback. The system supports fluid handoff between humans and agents through both natural language and direct manipulation of visualizations, while sandboxed execution ensures safe, reproducible workflows. In doing so, HiLSVA reframes agentic SciVis as a collaborative process that augments, rather than replaces, human analytical reasoning. We evaluate HiLSVA through representative case studies and a controlled user study with twelve participants of varying expertise across multiple autonomy settings. Results show that mixed-initiative interaction improves task completion, user control, and workflow transparency across different levels of user expertise, while revealing a tradeoff between execution efficiency and human oversight. These findings highlight the importance of human-centered design in agentic SciVis and guide the development of future collaborative visualization systems. We encourage readers to explore our demo video, case studies, and source code at https://hilsva.github.io/.

</details>


### [245] [Reviving Reflection-in-Action: Instilling Designerly Thinking in AI-Supported Ideation through Multimodal Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26626)
*Samangi Wadinambiarachchi,Jenny Waycott,Greg Wadley*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态提示（文本、草图、草图+标签）在AI创意支持工具中对设计专业学生的影响，发现草图模态能提升流畅性，但参与者仍偏好文本提示。作者讨论了如何通过设计AI工具促进学生反思性手绘技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI创意支持工具主要依赖文本提示生成方案型输出，但多模态提示（尤其是引入生产性摩擦以鼓励迭代和反思）在设计师-AI交互中的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 开发了原型AI-CST工具SketchifAI，采用混合方法、被试内设计，比较文本、草图、草图+标签三种输入模态对设计专业学生在意图表达能力、创造力支持感知和发散思维表现的影响。

Result: 草图模态倾向于增强流畅性，但在多样性、原创性或质量方面与文本模态相比证据不充分；然而参与者对文本提示表现出强烈偏好。

Conclusion: 讨论了如何设计AI工具以重新引入通过草图进行的反思，确保设计师-AI交互支持而非侵蚀学生的基本设计技能。

Abstract: Current AI-powered creativity support tools (AI-CSTs) primarily use text prompting to generate solution-oriented outputs. However, the potential value of multimodal prompting in designer-AI interaction, specifically the introduction of productive friction to encourage iteration and reflection, has not been fully explored. To address this, we developed SketchifAI, a prototype AI-CST, and evaluated it with design students. In a mixed-methods, within-participants study, we examined how different input modalities (text, sketch, and sketch-plus-tags) affected design students' perceived ability to express their intent, their perception of creativity support, and their divergent thinking performance. Our preliminary findings suggest that the sketch modality tended to enhance fluency, with inconclusive evidence for differences in variety, originality, or quality compared to text modality. Yet, paradoxically, participants showed a strong preference for text prompting. We discuss how AI tools might be designed to reintroduce reflection-through-sketching, ensuring that designer-AI interaction supports, rather than erodes, essential design skills in students.

</details>


### [246] [Invisible Impact of Empathy on Behavioral Change: Isolating the Effect of Empathy in Long-term Physical Activity Coaching Chatbot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26641)
*Li Siyan,Kai-Hui Liang,Shopnil Shahriar,Yilin Ye,Shiyoh Goetsu,Wei-Wei Du,Masahiro Yoshida,Tsunayuki Ohwa,Xuhai Xu,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建三个仅共情水平不同的WhatsApp身体活动教练聊天机器人，在六周内测实验（N=13）中发现：用户难以区分共情条件，非共情版本反而被认为更有用和吸引人，但高共情版本能带来更大的步数增长和更快的意图改善。这表明共情的作用是微妙的——用户难以明确识别，但能影响动机和信任以支持持续改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话系统（尤其大语言模型驱动的系统）通常默认将共情视为必要元素，但缺乏对其真实影响（尤其在行为改变领域，动机和依从性取决于微妙的用户-聊天机器人互动）的严格评估。作者希望通过实证检验这一假设。

Method: 在WhatsApp平台上构建三个身体活动教练聊天机器人，仅在共情水平（高、中、无）上有差异，开展为期六周的组内实验（N=13），收集用户区分能力、参与度评分、步数变化、意图改善等指标。

Result: 参与者难以区分不同共情条件；非共情版本在参与度和有用性评分上更高；但高共情版本与更大的平均步数增长和更快的意图改善相关。

Conclusion: 共情的作用具有微妙性：外行用户难以明确识别其存在，但它仍能影响动机和信任以支持持续性行为改变。作者通过精细加工可能性模型的外围路径解释这一模式，并为下一代身体活动教练聊天机器人的设计提出了平衡有效性与人性化连接的启示。

Abstract: Current dialogue systems, powered by large language models, often treat empathy as essential without assessing its true impact, especially in behavior change, where motivation and adherence often depend on subtle user-chatbot dynamics. We examine this assumption by building three WhatsApp physical-activity (PA) coaching chatbots that differ only in empathy level and evaluating them in a six-week within-subject study (N = 13). Participants struggled to distinguish between the empathy conditions, and the non-empathetic version was often rated as more engaging and useful. However, higher-empathy variants were still associated with a larger overall average increase in step counts and faster improvement in intention to follow advice. These results suggest empathy's role is nuanced: it may be hard for lay users to identify explicitly, but it can still shape motivation and trust that support sustained change. We interpret this pattern through the Elaboration Likelihood Model's peripheral route. We highlight design implications for building next-generation PA coaching chatbots that balance effectiveness with human-like connection.

</details>


### [247] [From Content to Strategy: Understanding the Motivations, Processes, and Impacts of AI-Guided Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26672)
*Chang Wan,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对26名使用AI制定沟通策略的用户进行深度访谈，发现AI引导的沟通（AI-guided communication）能促进自我反思、情绪平复、预防冲突升级、提供多元视角和安全空间，增强共情与沟通能力，但用户也担忧自我怀疑和失去独特性，对长期关系影响看法不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的进步已从单纯生成内容扩展到引导个体的沟通策略，但学界对这一新兴使用模式及其后果的理论与实证理解仍然有限，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 对26名曾使用AI制定沟通策略的用户进行深度访谈。

Result: 参与者强烈偏好使用AI分析亲密关系中的挑战性场景，因为AI能促进自我反思、缓解情绪、防止冲突升级、提供多元视角及安全的非评判性自我表露空间；同时参与者报告AI引导增强了共情与沟通技能，但也有人出现自我怀疑和对失去独特性的担忧；对长期关系影响的看法取决于AI在解决短期人际挑战中的感知有用性。

Conclusion: AI引导的沟通在亲密关系场景中具有积极作用，但也伴随自我认同方面的隐忧，长期影响尚需进一步研究。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-mediated communication (AI-MC) is conceptualized as applying AI to augment or generate message content (Hancock et al., 2020). However, advances in generative AI have expanded its use beyond generating content to guiding individuals' communication strategies, that is, AI-guided communication, yet theoretical and empirical understandings of this emerging use pattern and its consequences remains limited. To address this gap, this study conducted 26 in-depth interviews with individuals who have used AI to develop their communication strategies. Findings suggest participants strongly preferred using AI to analyze challenging scenarios in close relationships, because it fostered self-reflection, eased emotions, prevented conflict escalation, offered multiple perspectives, and provided a safe, nonjudgmental space for self-disclosure. Participants also stated that AI-guided communication enhanced their empathy and communication skills, though some voiced self-doubt and worried about losing their uniqueness. Views on long-term relational impact were mixed, depending on perceived usefulness of AI for resolving short-term interpersonal challenges.

</details>


### [248] [Modeling Adaptive Visual Search in Semantically Hierarchical Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26725)
*Saku Sourulahti,Jussi P. P. Jokinen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算认知模型，利用计算理性解释信息分组如何影响视觉搜索，通过语义与空间分组的对齐提升搜索效率，并用人类实验数据验证了模型在任务时长和眼动模式上的表现，展示了其在UI线框快速原型评估中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究信息分组（尤其是语义分组）如何影响视觉搜索效率，为UI设计提供认知层面的理论依据和快速评估工具。

Method: 基于计算理性框架构建认知模型，将人类行为视为对认知和任务约束的适应；利用层次化任务表征，结合语义与视觉结构；通过两项人类实验（视觉搜索与语义分类）验证模型，复现任务时长和眼动模式。

Result: 语义分组在与空间分组对齐时能显著提升搜索效率；模型成功复现了人类被试的任务时长和眼动模式。

Conclusion: 该模型扩展了现有视觉搜索模型，能反映层次化记忆结构在认知中的作用，并可用于UI线框的快速原型评估，为复杂真实场景的界面设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces a computational cognitive model to investigate how information grouping impacts visual search, a key consideration in user interface design. The model uses computational rationality to view user behavior as an adaptation to cognitive and task constraints. Our work highlights that humans use hierarchical task representations, exploiting semantic and visual structures to improve search efficiency within the constraints of the visual system. We validate this model with data from two human studies focused on visual search and semantic categorization, demonstrating that semantic grouping improves search performance when it aligns with spatial grouping. Our model replicates task durations and eye movement patterns. By improving understanding of how hierarchical memory structures are utilized in human cognition, the model extends previous visual search models. We showcase our model in the rapid prototyping and evaluation of semantic visual groupings within user interface wireframes, suggesting a pathway toward applications in more complex, real-world interface design.

</details>


### [249] ['A bit of chaos and madness': The AI Assessment Scale and the work of assessment reform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26729)
*Mike Perkins,Darius Postma,Jasper Roe,Susan Sisay,Craig Holdcroft*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对越南一所私立国际大学和英国一所公立大学的学术人员焦点小组访谈，探讨了人工智能评估量表（AIAS）在高等教育中的实施经验。研究发现，AIAS有助于建立共同语言、规范生成式AI使用边界并促进评估反思，但实施效果受治理、工具获取、教师信心、工作量、诚信问题、学科情境及学习目标一致性等因素影响。AIAS可能促进真实评估设计，但若脱离学习目标、学科情境和教师能力，则可能沦为合规性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI对大学评估设计带来压力，需在保持诚信、公平和有效性的同时重新设计评估框架。尽管AIAS等框架被提出，但关于教师如何实施这些框架的经验证据仍然有限，本研究旨在填补这一实证空白。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，在越南一所私立国际大学和英国一所公立大学进行5个焦点小组（共30名学术人员）数据收集，使用混合主题分析，并以批判性AI素养作为敏感化概念。

Result: 识别出六个主题：识别与整合AI、促进条件、能力建设、采纳路径、实践伦理及教学重构。教师重视AIAS作为共享语言，但实施受治理、工具获取、信心、工作量、诚信、学科及学习目标等因素制约。

Conclusion: AIAS能促进真实评估设计和学生参与，但当与学习目标、学科情境和教师能力脱节时，可能沦为合规性工具。研究为生成式AI评估框架从政策采纳到教学实施的制度条件提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) has intensified pressure on universities to redesign assessment while maintaining integrity, equity, and validity. Structured frameworks such as the Artificial Intelligence Assessment Scale (AIAS) offer one response, but evidence of how staff experience their implementation remains limited. This qualitative study examines AIAS implementation at a private international university in Vietnam and a public university in the United Kingdom. Data from five focus groups with 30 academic staff were analysed using hybrid thematic analysis, with Critical AI Literacy used as a sensitising concept. Six themes were developed: recognising and integrating AI, facilitating conditions, building capacity, pathways to adoption, ethics in practice, and reframing pedagogy.
  Staff valued the AIAS as a shared language for legitimising GenAI use, clarifying boundaries, and prompting reflection on assessment design. However, implementation was shaped by governance, tool access, staff confidence, workload, integrity concerns, disciplinary context, and alignment with learning outcomes. The findings show that the AIAS could prompt authentic assessment design and student engagement, but may become a compliance layer when disconnected from learning outcomes, disciplinary context, and staff capacity. This study contributes empirical evidence on the institutional conditions through which GenAI assessment frameworks move from policy adoption to pedagogical enactment.

</details>


### [250] [MedSWFlow: An Open-Source LLM Workflow for Drafting Medical Social Work Case Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26884)
*Yulin Mao,Shiyu Li,Shuping Song,Yuling Zhang,Yajun Song*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MedSWFlow 是一个开源、模型无关的大语言模型工作流，用于起草医疗社会工作计划，将案例规划任务分解为六个标准化阶段，并生成可供从业者审查的评估表格和服务计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗社会工作计划编制是一项复杂且高度专业化的任务，现有方法缺乏标准化和可复现的自动化辅助工具，亟需一个开源、模型无关的框架来提升案例规划的效率和一致性。

Method: 将专业案例规划任务转化为六个标准化阶段（评估、问题分析、目标设定、干预规划、风险预判、预期效果评估），利用成熟的社会工作和行为框架标准化案例输入，构建结构化案例档案，并通过分阶段提示生成可审查的评估表单和服务计划。

Result: 开发了一个开源研究框架，支持跨大语言模型提供商的可复现案例计划生成，输出可作为从业者审查的草案。

Conclusion: MedSWFlow 作为从业者审查的辅助工具，输出为草案而非最终服务决策，为医疗社会工作计划自动化提供了标准化和可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: We present MedSWFlow, an open-source, model-agnostic LLM workflow for drafting medical social work case plans. The framework translates professional case-planning tasks into six stages: assessment, problem analysis, goal setting, intervention planning, risk anticipation, and planned effect evaluation. Drawing on established social work and behavioral frameworks, MedSWFlow standardizes case inputs, builds structured case profiles, and generates reviewable assessment forms and service plans through staged prompting. The system is released as an open-source research framework for reproducible case-plan generation across LLM providers. Outputs are intended as practitioner-reviewed drafts rather than final service decisions. Source code: https://github.com/santhiyacw-droid/MedSWFlow/tree/main.

</details>


### [251] [Optimizing Human-Machine Interface for Real-Time AI Support in the Operating Room: the CVS Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26886)
*Lorenzo Arboit,Nicolas Chanel,Aditya Murali,Pietro Mascagni,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过混合方法用户中心设计研究，优化了腹腔镜胆囊切除术中AI辅助安全手术的人机界面，开发了名为CVS Copilot的外科医生控制、角色自适应的HMI系统，支持最少默认可视化和可选叠加显示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统用于腹腔镜胆囊切除术中自动关键安全视野评估正接近临床转化，但临床安全性和有效性不仅取决于算法性能，还取决于人机界面的质量。本研究旨在探究术中应如何呈现和控制AI生成的预测结果。

Method: 17名外科医生（包括住院医师、主治医师和教授）参与混合方法用户中心设计研究，通过访谈探索交互模式、辅助时机、可视化策略和控制机制，采用反思性主题分析和人因启发式方法进行分析。

Result: 大多数外科医生支持术中AI决策辅助但拒绝自主决策；主治医师偏好关键时刻的最小AI反馈，住院医师偏好带置信度指示的可选引导；外科医生一致优先选择可视、外科医生控制、最小侵入性显示；最强支持为最小叠加显示和按需解剖分割；对持续叠加、触觉反馈和数字置信度显示存在担忧。

Conclusion: 研究结果指导设计了CVS Copilot——一种外科医生控制、角色自适应的人机界面，提供基于AI的CVS评估，采用最少默认可视化和可选叠加显示。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems for automated Critical View of Safety (CVS) assessment in laparoscopic cholecystectomy are nearing clinical translation. Beyond algorithmic performance, clinical safety and effectiveness depend on the quality of the human-machine interface (HMI). This work examines how AI-generated predictions should be presented and controlled intraoperatively. Seventeen surgeons, including residents, attending surgeons, and professors, took part in a mixed-methods, user-centered design study to optimize an intraoperative HMI for AI-assisted safe laparoscopic cholecystectomy. Interviews explored interaction modalities, timing of assistance, visualization strategies, and control mechanisms across surgical roles, and were analyzed using reflexive thematic analysis and human-factors heuristics. Most surgeons (16/17) supported the use of AI for intraoperative decision support while rejecting autonomous decision-making. Attendings preferred minimal AI feedback at decisive moments (13/14), whereas residents favored optional guidance (3/3) with confidence indicators and on-demand anatomical overlays. Across interviews, surgeons consistently prioritized visual, surgeon-controlled, minimally intrusive displays, with the strongest support for a minimal overlay (16/17) and on-demand anatomical segmentation (13/17). Recurrent concerns included persistent overlays, haptic feedback, and numeric confidence displays, although these were not uniformly raised across the cohort. These findings informed the design of CVS Copilot, a surgeon-controlled, role-adaptive HMI that provides AI-based CVS assessment with minimal default visualization and optional overlays.

</details>


### [252] [Game Changers: Designing and Measuring Dynamic Feedback To Help Users Self-Regulate in a VR Pointing Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26925)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Scott Bateman,Iris Brunner,Lars Evald,Hendrik Knoche*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过虚拟现实指向任务，系统研究了三种性能指标（短完成时间、直路径、高峰值速度）在不同时间点（持续、动作结束时、任务结束时）驱动的动态反馈对玩家表现和感知的影响。研究发现动态反馈平均能帮助玩家更直、更快地指向，但对部分玩家效果较小甚至相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态反馈对玩家在核心游戏任务（如指向、操控）中的表现、学习和提升至关重要，但性能指标如何影响玩家的表现和感知尚不明确。

Method: 采用虚拟现实指向任务，系统探究三种性能指标（短完成时间、直路径、高峰值速度）驱动的反馈在不同时间点（持续、动作结束时、任务结束时）对玩家的影响，并进行定量比较。

Result: 动态反馈平均上帮助玩家指向更直、更快，但对部分玩家效果较小或相反。研究对三种反馈形式与指标驱动的关系进行了定量对比。

Conclusion: 研究为游戏设计师提供了动态反馈设计的依据，帮助其了解何时采用与期望游戏表现目标一致的反馈方案。

Abstract: The way games dynamically convey information through feedback is critical to players' ability to perform, learn, and improve. However, it is poorly understood how performance metrics impact player performance and perception in core game tasks like pointing or steering.
