With a virtual reality pointing task we systematically explored how three performance metrics driving the feedback affected players when rewarding short completion times, straight movements, or high peak speed. across different points in time - continuously, at end-of-action, or at end-of-task. On average the dynamic feedback helped people point more straight and faster, while for others it had small or opposite effect. The study quantitatively compared dynamic feedback across three forms with the metrics driving the form as the intended locus of quantitative comparison. Our work improves game designers basis for crafting dynamic feedback by helping them know when to employ feedback schemes that align with desirable game performance objectives.

</details>


### [253] [Continuous Behavioral Synthesis for Adaptive Health Dashboards: An LLM-Mediated Architecture Integrating Explicit Preference, Spatial Reorganization, and Attention Allocation Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26937)
*Tiziano Santilli,Mina Alipour,Mahyar T. Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种利用大语言模型作为行为合成引擎的技术架构，通过整合三种稀疏、异构的用户信号（显性微反馈、空间优先级、注意力投入），在结构化提示工程框架下实现自适应用户界面的即时个性化，无需大量历史数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应界面依赖基于规则的系统（编码设计师直觉）或需要大量历史数据的机器学习方法，难以从稀疏、异构的用户信号中实现即时个性化。

Method: 构建三层架构：(1) 集成三种行为通道（显性微反馈、拖拽交互的空间优先级、悬停时间的注意力投入）；(2) 分层提示构建方法，分离时间上下文确定、行为信号提取、显式偏好执行和用户画像合成；(3) 结合手动指定的行为解释与时间启发式，通过LLM调解合成多信号。

Result: 在个人健康监控领域实例化，管理14项健康指标及7种可视化模态，通过多场景适应行为分析、工作实现以及代表性后适应截图展示系统效果。

Conclusion: 该方法通过LLM调和多种用户信号，解决了将低级交互模式转化为高级设计决策的技术难题，实现了无需大量数据的即时界面自适应。

Abstract: The engineering of adaptive user interfaces has traditionally relied on either rule-based systems encoding designer intuitions about user needs or machine learning approaches requiring substantial historical data before achieving effective personalization. We present a technical architecture that leverages Large Language Models as behavioral synthesis engines to enable immediate adaptation from sparse, heterogeneous user signals. Our system integrates three distinct behavioral channels, i) explicit micro-feedback on individual interface elements, ii) spatial priority inferred from manual widget reorganization through drag-and-drop interaction, iii) and attentional investment measured through dwell time during hover events, within a structured prompt engineering framework that continuously regenerates dashboard layouts while maintaining explanatory coherence. The architecture addresses the technical challenge of translating low-level interaction patterns into high-level design decisions through a layered prompt construction methodology that separates temporal context determination, behavioral signal extraction, explicit preference enforcement, and user profile synthesis. The approach combines manually specified behavioral interpretations and temporal heuristics with LLM-mediated synthesis, enabling the reconciliation of multiple simultaneous signals that would be difficult to encode through explicit rules alone. We demonstrate the system through an instantiation in the personal health monitoring domain, including an analytical evaluation of adaptation behavior across multiple scenarios and a working implementation managing fourteen distinct health metrics across seven widget visualization modalities. The evaluation compares profile-driven initialization, multi-signal behavioral adaptation, and presents the resulting interfaces through representative post-adaptation screenshots.

</details>


### [254] [What Holds Back Brain-Computer Interfaces? Uncovering Challenges and Opportunities in BCI-controlled Games for Cerebral Palsy Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26951)
*Bastian Ilsø Hougaard,Kirstine Schultz Dalgaard,Kirstine Johanne Stougaard Klebæk,Hendrik Knoche,Mads Jochumsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于脑机接口的脑瘫康复游戏设计，通过临床治疗师和十名脑瘫患者的初步反馈，发现游戏中不规律的帮助虽缓解单调性，但会引发对自主控制感的怀疑；治疗师认为脑机接口康复可作为传统训练的补充，帮助患者从趣味练习过渡到自主训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑机接口为脑瘫康复提供了有前景的途径，但基于BCI的脑瘫康复设计约束尚不明确，尤其是脑瘫患者如何通过BCI体验控制感以及如何体验计算机介导的游戏辅助机制尚不清楚。

Method: 基于来自一位脑瘫治疗师的临床见解和十名脑瘫患者参与BCI游戏原型的体验报告，收集初步临床和用户视角。

Result: BCI游戏中不规律的帮助缓解了单调性，但也引发了对自主控制感的怀疑；治疗师认为BCI康复可作为传统训练的补充。

Conclusion: 提出了关键挑战和机遇，以指导和赋能BCI训练在脑瘫康复中的进一步设计和研究。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) offer promising avenues for cerebral palsy (CP) rehabilitation at home and in the clinic, using games that promote engagement and sustained training effort. Nonetheless, the design constraints of BCI-based CP rehabilitation remain unclear, especially how individuals with CP experience a sense of control through BCI, and how they experience computer-mediated game assistance. To address this gap, we present preliminary clinical and user perspectives on BCI-based CP rehabilitation, drawing on in-clinic insights from a CP therapist and experiential accounts from ten individuals with CP engaging with BCI game prototypes. Sporadic help in BCI games eased monotony, but also fostered doubts regarding agency. The therapist saw BCI rehabilitation as complementary to traditional training, facilitating the transition from playful exercises to autonomous, self-managed training. We outline key challenges and opportunities to inform and empower further design and research of BCI training for CP.

</details>


### [255] [Floor Raiser or Ceiling Limiter? Differential Storytelling Outcomes with a Child-Centric GenAI System Across Individual Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27067)
*Min Fan,Wanqing Ma,Xinyue Cui,Xiaolu Dai,Shengyu Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过40名7-12岁儿童的实验，发现GenAI辅助故事创作能缩小质量差距83.5%，但主要体现在创造力和丰富性维度，对连贯性和叙事结构帮助有限；同时不同年龄段儿童的语义选择偏好存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI有望普及创意素养，但尚不清楚是否所有儿童都能平等受益，需要实证研究验证。

Method: 采用混合方法被试内实验设计，40名2-6年级儿童（7-12岁）比较GenAI辅助与传统故事板两种条件。

Result: 1) GenAI辅助导致"抬升底部"的趋同模式，质量差距缩小83.5%，但仅改善创造力和丰富性；2) 低龄儿童倾向选择语义距离大的关键词，年长儿童倾向选择语义接近的关键词；3) 图像再生与结构质量维度正相关，但基线控制后关联减弱。

Conclusion: 提出"机制依存的支架式"设计原则，用于构建适应多样性儿童的GenAI故事系统。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) holds promise for democratizing creative literacy, yet whether it benefits all children equally remains unclear. Using a child-centric GenAI storytelling system for children aged 7-12, we conducted a mixed-methods within-subjects experiment (N = 40, Grades 2-6) comparing GenAI-assisted and traditional storyboard conditions. Three findings emerged. First, the GenAI-assisted condition was associated with a floor-raising convergence pattern, with the quality gap narrowing by 83.5%, driven by lower-end support and upper-end constraint mechanisms. This convergence was dimension-selective, improving creativity and richness while leaving coherence and narrative structure tied to baseline performance. Second, younger children more often selected semantically distant keywords while older children preferred semantically closer ones, although engagement orientation varied across individuals regardless of age. Third, image regeneration was positively associated with structural quality dimensions, though this association was attenuated after baseline control. We propose mechanism-contingent scaffolding as a design principle for adaptive GenAI storytelling systems serving diverse children.

</details>


### [256] [Urban Context and Travel Experience Events: An Exploratory Comparison of Two German Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27077)
*Marie Güntert,Esther Bosch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过移动应用追踪德国汉堡（城市）和图特林根（乡村）两地91名参与者的日常出行，运用多层回归分析发现：城市区域准点性缺失和负面信息事件影响最大，乡村区域运力充足性正向影响最强、准点性缺失和个人状态差负向影响最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究影响公共交通出行体验的关键事件因素，并比较城市与乡村地区的差异，为改善地方公共交通提供决策依据。

Method: 使用移动应用采集21名（图特林根）和70名（汉堡）参与者的日常出行追踪数据，收集实时体验评价和情境信息，采用多层回归分析评估准点性、运力、信息事件等因素对途中体验的影响。

Result: 图特林根：运力充足正向影响最强，准点性缺失和个人状态差负向影响最强；汉堡：准点性缺失和负面信息事件影响最大。

Conclusion: 识别出的关键影响因素可为改善地方公共交通的决策和措施提供基础。

Abstract: The presented study investigates events influencing public transportation experience in both urban (Hamburg) and rural (Tuttlingen) areas in Germany, with the aim of identifying events that affect travel experience and as a result travel behavior. Using a mobile application, 21 participants in Tuttlingen and 70 participants in Hamburg tracked everyday trips, providing real-time evaluations of travel experiences along with situational data. Multi-level regression analyses were applied to assess the impact of events such as punctuality, capacity offer, information about public transportation and others on the ontrip experience. Results indicate that a sufficient public transportation capacity offer has the strongest positive effect in Tuttlingen, whereas a lack of punctuality and low personal well-being have the strongest negative effects. In Hamburg, a lack of punctuality and a negative information event have the largest impacts. These identified effects provide a foundation for decision-making and measures to improve local public transportation.

</details>


### [257] [Behind the Mask: A Taxonomic Analysis of Activities in Online Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27111)
*Debora F De Souza,Gabriela Beltrao,Berta Chulvi,Sergio Dantonio,Mehmet Gokay Ozerim,Javier Torregrosa,Adrian Giron,Angel Panizo,Pablo Miralles Gonzalez,Helena Liz,Javier Huertas Tato,Sonia Sousa,Alejandro Martin,Monika Maciuliene,David Camacho*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过与领域专家合作和文献综述，设计了一个恶意行为者分类体系，用于区分在线社交网络中传播虚假信息的恶意行为者的特征、活动和策略，并通过反移民话语案例研究验证其应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络中虚假信息的传播日益严重，现有研究缺乏对恶意行为者系统性的分类框架，难以有效识别和区分不同行为者的特征与策略。

Method: 结合领域专家协作与学术文献综述，设计恶意行为者分类体系；通过案例研究（分析社交媒体中反移民话语）验证分类体系的应用效果。

Result: 提出了一个可区分恶意行为者特征、活动与策略的分类体系，并在反移民话语案例中成功应用，展示了该分类体系的实际效用。

Conclusion: 该分类体系有助于研究人员和实践者在网络系统中进行负责任的系统设计，以应对虚假信息传播问题。

Abstract: The broadcast of disinformation in online social networks (OSN) is a growing concern examined across several disciplines, including human-computer interaction (HCI). The pervasive issue has been prompting novel approaches to identify the malicious actors behind the dissemination of deceptive and fabricated content. Analyzing the characteristics and activities of these actors, we designed a taxonomy informed by collaboration with subject matter experts (SMEs) and a review of the academic literature. Our study explores how to distinguish the characteristics, activities, and strategies of malicious actors on OSN and examines how they contribute to the spread of disinformation. We describe the design process and the application of the taxonomy in a case study analyzing anti-migration discourse in social media channels, and reflect on its potential to aid researchers and practitioners in the responsible design of network systems.

</details>


### [258] ["Everyone Says Them": Deception Typologies, Probabilistic Trust, and Grassroots Safety Knowledge Among Gay Dating App Users in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27284)
*Yibo Meng,Lyumanshan Ye,Yingfangzhong Sun,Bingyi Liu,Huidi Lu,Xiaolan Ding*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对22名中国男同性恋用户的半结构化访谈，揭示了同志交友应用中超越个人资料虚假的多种欺骗类型（关系、情感、财务、商业），展示了用户形成的多层概率性验证策略，并说明了风险识别是一种由经验流通、策略抽象化和规则编码共同塑造的社区协作实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同志交友应用已成为性少数男性寻求关系和社区的关键平台，但这些平台中的欺骗性互动在HCI和CSCW研究中仍未被充分探索，作者旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈方法，对22名来自Blued、Aloha、Fanka、Soul等平台的中国男同性恋用户进行质性研究。

Result: 识别出超越个人资料虚假的欺骗类型学（关系、情感、财务、商业欺骗）；发现用户形成多层概率性验证策略，信任评估是多信号、暂时性的过程而非二元判断；揭示风险识别是通过经验流通、策略抽象化和社区规则编码形成的协作实践。

Conclusion: 同志交友应用中的欺骗行为复杂多样，用户通过社区协作和长期平台使用发展出精细的验证策略，对平台设计和社区治理具有重要启示。

Abstract: Gay dating applications have become critical platforms for sexual minority men to seek relationships and community, yet they also expose users to deceptive interactions that remain underexplored in HCI and CSCW research. This study examines how gay male users in China experience, identify, and respond to deception on dating applications. Through semi-structured interviews with 22 participants across platforms including Blued, Aloha, Fanka, and Soul, we make three contributions. First, we identify a typology of deceptive practices extending beyond profile misrepresentation to encompass relational, emotional, financial, and commercial forms of deception. Second, we document the layered, probabilistic verification strategies users develop through long-term platform use, showing that trust assessment operates as a multi-signal, provisional process rather than a binary judgment. Third, we demonstrate that risk recognition is a collaborative practice shaped by the circulation of experience, the abstraction of recurrent tactics, and the codification of shared rules within the community.

</details>


### [259] [Reading the Same Data Differently: Interpretive Labor Across System Boundaries in Electronic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27301)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Zhiqi Gao,Shuai Ma,Hongyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文研究了社区矫正中电子监控(EM)作为双面传感系统时，监管人员与被监管者之间由于数据获取、上下文信息和推理过程的不对称导致"解释性错位"现象，以及这种行为如何影响双方的行为策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注EM作为刑事司法工具或合规机制，但对传感数据如何在日常实践中产生意义知之甚少，特别是双面系统中不同角色对同一数据流的理解差异。

Method: 基于对中国社区矫正系统中26名被监管者和12名监管人员的半结构化访谈，分析双方如何从各自立场推理同一数据流。

Result: 发现被监管者通过结果推断系统逻辑但难以了解数据解释过程，监管者则利用情境知识、专业经验和制度程序从模糊痕迹重建行为；双方之间存在"解释性错位"。

Conclusion: 提出CSCW视角下的连续传感作为分布式解释性工作，并识别了使数据到决策过程更可读、可争议、可问责的设计机会。

Abstract: Electronic monitoring (EM) systems are increasingly used in community corrections to enforce spatial, temporal, and behavioral rules through continuous sensing. While prior work has examined EM as a criminal justice tool or as a mechanism for compliance, less is known about how sensed data become meaningful in everyday practice. This poster examines EM as a dual-sided sensing system in which supervised individuals and authorities reason about the same data stream from different positions. Based on semi-structured interviews with 26 supervised individuals and 12 authorities in China's community corrections system, we show that supervised individuals infer system logic from outcomes with limited visibility into how data are interpreted, while authorities reconstruct behavior from ambiguous traces using contextual knowledge, professional experience, and institutional procedures. We call this structural divergence interpretive misalignment. It emerges from asymmetric access to data, context, and reasoning processes, and it shapes behavior through probing, strategic adaptation, over-compliance, disengagement, and contestation. We contribute a CSCW account of continuous sensing as distributed interpretive work and identify design opportunities for making data-to-decision processes more legible, contestable, and accountable across system sides.

</details>


### [260] [AI Healthcare Chatbots as Information Infrastructure: A Large-Scale Study of User-Reported Breakdowns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27302)
*Muhammad Hassan,Ramazan Yener,Ece Gumusel,Masooda Bashir*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究分析了59款AI医疗聊天机器人的15,000+条用户评论，通过主题建模和解释性分析识别出三类主要故障：访问障碍与服务不可靠、用户体验与交互质量、计费与客服问题。隐私和安全问题与最负面的用户体验相关。将AI医疗聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，揭示了访问、可用性和信任方面的失败如何影响用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI医疗聊天机器人日益广泛用于支持健康信息检索和自我管理，但其性能和对用户的影响尚待深入研究。本文旨在通过大规模用户评论分析，系统性地理解这类系统在日常信息性和情感性情境中的实际运作情况。

Method: 收集59款AI医疗聊天机器人应用的15,000+条用户评论，采用主题建模（Topic Modeling）和解释性分析（Interpretive Analysis）方法进行数据处理和模式识别。

Result: 识别出三类反复出现的系统故障：1) 访问障碍和服务不可靠；2) 用户体验和交互质量；3) 计费和客户支持问题。其中隐私和安全问题与最负面的用户体验高度相关。

Conclusion: 将AI医疗聊天机器人视为信息基础设施，指出访问、可用性和信任方面的失败对用户产生显著影响，为设计师、政策制定者和信息专业人员改进数字健康系统提供了可操作的洞察。

Abstract: AI healthcare chatbots are increasingly used to support health information seeking and self-management, yet their performance and impact on users remains to be studied. This study examines over 15,000 user reviews from 59 AI healthcare chatbot apps to explore how these systems function in everyday informational and emotional contexts. Topic modeling and interpretive analysis identify three recurring breakdowns: access barriers and service unreliability, user experience and interaction quality, and billing and customer support issues. Privacy and security concerns are associated with the most negative experiences. By framing AI healthcare chatbots as information infrastructures, our findings highlight how failures in access, usability, and trust affect users, offering actionable insights for designers, policymakers, and information professionals aiming to improve digital health systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Unsupervised Memory-Enhanced Video Transformers: Obstacle Detection for Autonomous Agricultural Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26151)
*Théo Biardeau,Anne-Sophie Capelle-Laizé,Salwan Alwan,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VMTAD，一种基于Transformer和记忆模块的完全无监督异常检测方法，用于农业机器人在动态场景下的实时障碍物检测，在油菜籽数据集上达到SOTA性能（检测AUROC 0.973，分割AUROC 0.997），轻量版推理仅需14ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业机器人安全传感器（如LiDAR）无法检测植物冠层下方的障碍物，而基于监督学习的视觉方法对训练集中未出现的障碍物泛化性差；无监督异常检测在动态场景（移动机器人视角）中表现不佳，亟需一种能处理动态上下文且无需标注数据的实时障碍物检测方法。

Method: VMTAD采用Transformer架构，并创新性地引入专用记忆模块，通过处理前序帧的编码表示来利用时间上下文信息，从而有效应对机器人运动导致的动态场景变化。模型仅使用正常操作的图像进行无监督训练，无需任何数据标签。

Result: 在"Grillion"农业机器人的油菜籽数据集上，VMTAD达到检测AUROC 0.973、分割AUROC 0.997的SOTA性能；轻量变体在保持高精度的同时推理时间仅需14ms，满足机器人总制动距离对实时性的要求。

Conclusion: VMTAD通过记忆增强的Transformer架构有效解决了动态农业场景下的无监督异常检测问题，在精度和实时性之间取得了良好平衡，为农业机器人的安全自主导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While autonomous rovers have become indispensable to precision farming, achieving consistent operational safety remains a critical challenge. Conventional safety sensors, such as LiDAR, fail to detect obstacles positioned below the plant canopy, posing a significant risk. While camera-based supervised learning methods can detect common objects, they perform poorly when faced with obstacles that were not present in their training data. Actual unsupervised anomaly detection offers a solution by learning the normal visual patterns of an environment, but often fails for the dynamic scenes captured by a moving rover.\\ This paper introduces Video Memory Transformers for Anomaly Detection (VMTAD), a fully unsupervised method designed for real-time obstacle detection in dynamic agricultural scenes. VMTAD utilizes a transformer-driven architecture augmented with a dedicated memory module. This memory module leverages temporal context by processing encoded representations of preceding frames. This approach enables the system to effectively address the dynamic context caused by the robot's movement. The model is trained using only images that represent normal operation, requiring no data labels.\\ VMTAD was rigorously evaluated on the 'Grillion' agricultural rover. On a challenging rapeseed dataset, VMTAD achieved state-of-the-art performance, reaching a 0.973 detection and 0.997 segmentation Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve. A lightweight variant provides an optimal balance of high accuracy and real-time inference (14 ms), which is critical for safety, as confirmed by our analysis of the rover's total stopping distance.

</details>


### [262] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Autonomous Microrobot Navigation and Intervention in Simulated Blood Capillaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26154)
*Jannik Drotleff,Samuel Tovey,Paul Hohenberger,Christoph Lohrmann,Julian Hoßbach,Konstantin Nikolaou,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于物理的毛细血管网络仿真环境，结合真实血流动力学、红细胞动力学和解剖分支结构，利用深度强化学习训练微型机器人通过趋化性导航。系统揭示了机器人尺寸与速度的物理极限，发现智能体自主学习多种通用策略（如旋转-奔跑、搜索-停留），且无需重新训练即可实现靶向阻塞与解堵毛细血管血流，恢复至健康基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究中的微型机器人导航局限于理想化几何环境，忽略了体内复杂的流体动力学流场、受限分支结构和密集细胞障碍，导致训练的控制策略难以直接应用于真实生物环境。

Method: 构建了物理真实的毛细血管网络仿真，包含流体动力学流场、显式红细胞动力学及基于解剖的分支几何结构；采用深度强化学习训练智能体通过趋化信号导航；系统扫描机器人尺寸与游泳速度参数空间。

Result: 发现存在由布朗运动和血流主导的"禁止区域"；智能体自主发现多种通用策略（run-and-rotate和search-and-sit）；训练后的智能体无需重新训练即可执行靶向阻塞与解堵，恢复血流至健康水平。

Conclusion: 强化学习是开发复杂生物环境中自主微型机器人干预策略的可行框架。

Abstract: Autonomous microrobots navigating biological vasculature could enable targeted drug delivery and thrombolysis, yet training control policies for realistic environments remains an open challenge. Prior reinforcement learning (RL) studies of microrobotic navigation have been limited to idealized geometries that omit complex hydrodynamic flow fields, confined branching structures, and dense cellular obstacles found in vivo. Here, we develop a physically grounded simulation of a blood capillary network, incorporating realistic hydrodynamic flow fields, explicit red blood cell dynamics, and anatomically derived branching geometry, and train deep RL agents to navigate it via chemotaxis. We systematically map the physical limits of navigation across robot size and swimming speed, revealing a forbidden regime where Brownian motion and flow overcome propulsion. Successful agents independently discover multiple universal strategy types, including run-and-rotate and energy-efficient search-and-sit policies, regardless of robot parameters. Without retraining, these agents perform targeted blocking and unblocking of capillary flow, restoring throughput to healthy baseline levels. These results establish RL as a viable framework for developing autonomous microrobotic intervention strategies in complex biological environments.

</details>


### [263] [RMTL: Reinforced Micro-task Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation with VLM Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26175)
*Anıl Can Ateş,Orhan Kahraman,Cihan Topal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出强化微任务学习（RMTL），通过将长时程操作任务分解为多个语言描述的微任务，并为每个微任务使用独立的VLM奖励提示，结合反向课程学习和分层策略，解决了单提示VLM奖励在复杂操作任务中信号平坦、难以学习的问题，显著提升了学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的零样本奖励模型使用单一全局提示，对于初始条件随机化的长时程操作任务过于粗糙，导致奖励信号在轨迹大部分阶段近乎平坦，智能体难以检测早期进步，学习效率低下。

Method: RMTL将操作任务分解为多个语言描述的微任务，每个微任务使用独立的VLM提示计算多视角奖励；采用反向课程学习逐步暴露更难初始状态；先用固定距离规则训练PPO工作器，再替换为学习型分层管理器实现完全学习化的分层策略。

Result: 在Fetch操作环境中，RMTL相比单提示VLM奖励提供了更信息丰富的奖励信号，实现了更快的学习速度。

Conclusion: 将VLM奖励分解为微任务特定的语言提示，可以显著提升语言引导强化学习在机器人操作任务中的可扩展性和学习效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation often requires manually designing a dense reward function, which is difficult to tune and often fragile, or learning a reward from human demonstrations or preferences, which can be expensive. A recent line of work uses pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-shot reward models, replacing these costs with a single text prompt. However, we argue that a single global prompt is too coarse for long-horizon manipulation tasks with randomized initial conditions. The single-prompt VLM reward is near-flat for much of the trajectory, making early progress hard for the agent to detect. We propose Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL), an approach that decomposes a manipulation task into a small set of language-described micro-tasks and trains the agent to switch between them. At each step, the agent receives a multi-view VLM reward computed using the prompt of the currently active micro-task and averaged across multiple camera views to reduce the effect of view-specific occlusions. A reverse curriculum gradually exposes the agent to harder initial conditions, while a PPO worker is first trained with a fixed distance-based rule that selects the active micro-task. We then replace this rule with a learned hierarchical manager, turning rule-based phase selection into a fully learned hierarchical policy. We instantiate RMTL on the Fetch manipulation environment using three short stage-specific prompts and without additional prompt tuning. Experiments show that RMTL provides more informative reward signals than single-prompt VLM rewards, enabling faster learning. These results suggest that decomposing VLM rewards into micro-task-specific language prompts can substantially improve the scalability of language-guided reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [264] [LiMoDE: Rethinking Lifelong Robot Manipulation from a Mixture-of-Dynamic-Experts Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26183)
*Zhihao Gu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LiMoDE（终身混合动态专家），一个两阶段学习框架用于终身机器人操作。第一阶段通过动态MoE结构进行多任务预训练学习先验知识，第二阶段通过终身MoE适应机制学习新专家并与冻结的旧专家动态组合，实现知识迁移。在仿真和真实任务上验证了其有效性和强终身适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个问题：1）参数高效微调虽缓解灾难性遗忘但无法提取可复用技能并建模技能间交互；2）近期工作尝试用提示学习解决但仍有不足。因此需要一个能从架构层面同时解决技能提取与技能间交互建模的终身学习方案。

Method: 两阶段学习方案：1）多任务预训练阶段提出动态MoE结构，根据运动信息激活不同数量的异构专家处理不同短期操作任务，学习先验知识；2）任务适应阶段设计终身MoE适应机制，学习终身专家并与冻结的旧专家动态组合，促进新任务的知识迁移。

Result: 在仿真终身学习基准和真实世界任务上均进行验证，实验表明该方法在引入适度额外可训练参数和推理开销的情况下，实现了优越的性能和强大的终身适应能力。

Conclusion: LiMoDE通过动态专家混合架构有效解决了终身机器人操作中的灾难性遗忘和知识迁移问题，是一种高效且实用的终身学习方案。

Abstract: Building a generalist robot that can leverage prior knowledge for continuous task adaptation remains a significant challenge. Previous works alleviate the catastrophic forgetting problem by parameter-efficient fine-tuning for single-task adaptation. However, they fail to extract reusable skills and model the interaction with other skills effectively. Recent works try to address these issues by learning prompts. Differently, this paper presents an architectural perspective on the Lifelong Mixture of Dynamic Experts (\textit{LiMoDE}), a novel two-stage learning scheme for lifelong robot manipulation. Specifically, a dynamic MoE structure is first proposed in the multi-task pre-training stage to learn prior knowledge, where a varied number of heterogeneous experts are activated based on the motion information to address different short-term manipulations. Subsequently, in the task adaptation stage, we design a lifelong MoE adaptation mechanism % (LiMoEAM) that learns lifelong experts and dynamically combines them with frozen ones for new tasks, facilitating the knowledge transfer during adaptation. The proposed \textit{LiMoDE} is evaluated on both the simulated lifelong learning benchmark and real-world tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving superior performance and strong lifelong adaptation by introducing a moderate number of additional trainable parameters and inference overhead.

</details>


### [265] [Morphology-Specific Closed-Loop Control of Logarithmic-Spiral Continuum Arms via Online Jacobian Error Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26188)
*Partha Datta,Yi Jin,Wei Lin,C. Chase Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对对数螺旋连续体臂的形态特异性闭环任务空间控制框架，基于分段肌腱驱动模型和解析雅可比矩阵，结合Broyden更新与卡尔曼滤波的在线误差补偿，在轨迹跟踪、姿态控制、抗扰动及多臂协同操作中显著优于PCC基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数螺旋形态在生物附属物中普遍存在，且已被用于连续体机械臂的抓取应用，但现有对数螺旋机器人缺乏逆运动学模型和闭环控制能力，限制了其精确操作与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出分段肌腱驱动中心线骨架连续体模型（MuJoCo实现），推导对数螺旋运动学的解析任务空间雅可比矩阵，结合Broyden割线更新与卡尔曼滤波进行在线雅可比误差补偿，实现连续校正模型误差的闭环控制器。

Result: 在平面和空间仿真中，方法在轨迹跟踪、姿态调节、扰动抑制、三维定位及位置-姿态联合控制中一致优于PCC基线，跟踪误差更小、姿态漂移更少、雅可比估计误差有界。成功应用于障碍辅助释放、自适应全臂抓取和多臂协同操作。

Conclusion: 将对数螺旋形态与在线雅可比补偿相结合，能够实现对高度欠驱动连续体机械臂的精确、鲁棒且可扩展的控制，为未来硬件实现和增强学习控制建立了基于物理的基准。

Abstract: Logarithmic spirals are ubiquitous in biological appendages and provide an attractive morphology for continuum manipulators capable of reaching, wrapping, and grasping. Recently reported logarithmic-spiral robots demonstrated scalable fabrication and versatile grasping but lacked inverse kinematics and closed-loop control. This work presents the first morphology-specific closed-loop task-space control framework for logarithmic-spiral continuum arms. A segmented tendon-driven model with a centerline backbone and equilateral tendon routing is developed in MuJoCo to capture tapered compliance and contact dynamics. An analytical task-space Jacobian is derived directly from the logarithmic-spiral kinematics and combined with online Jacobian error compensation using a Broyden secant update and Kalman-filter estimation. The resulting controller continuously corrects modeling errors arising from nonlinear deformation, contact, and geometric mismatch. The framework is validated through planar and spatial simulations, including trajectory tracking, attitude regulation, disturbance rejection, three-dimensional position tracking, and simultaneous position-orientation control. Compared with a piecewise-constant-curvature (PCC) baseline, the proposed method consistently reduces tracking errors, suppresses attitude drift, and maintains a bounded Jacobian estimation error. The controller is further applied to morphology-enabled manipulation tasks, including obstacle-assisted reach-wrap-release motions, adaptive whole-arm grasping, and cooperative multi-arm object handling. Results demonstrate that combining logarithmic-spiral morphology with online Jacobian compensation enables accurate, robust, and scalable control of highly underactuated continuum manipulators. The proposed framework establishes a physics-grounded baseline for future hardware implementation and learning-augmented soft robotic control.

</details>


### [266] [OmniContact: Chaining Meta-Skills via Contact Flow for Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26201)
*Runyi Yu,Xiaoyi Lin,Ji Ma,Yinhuai Wang,Koukou Luo,Jiahao Ji,Huayi Wang,Wenjia Wang,Runhan Zhang,Ping Tan,Ting Wu,Ruoli Dai,Qifeng Chen,Lei Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于接触流(Contact Flow)的分层框架，用于人形机器人长时域移动操作，包含低层策略CF-Track和高层模块CF-Gen，配合OmniContact数据集，在长时域任务中显著优于基线方法，并支持与VLM集成进行语义任务分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在显式人-物交互表示虽然精确但难以用于高层规划，隐式技能嵌入紧凑但缺乏可解释性，无法实现可靠的技能组合与自主恢复。

Method: 提出以接触流(CF)为核心的分层框架：低层策略CF-Track学习统一的操作技能库，高层模块CF-Gen启发式合成未来接触流序列，并收集基于动作捕捉的OmniContact数据集。

Result: 在Carry Box任务上达到98.7%成功率，Push-Stack Boxes任务上达到76.5%成功率，在元技能和技能链接上分别平均超越基线40.9%和66.5%。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅能实现鲁棒执行、自主故障恢复和灵活技能组合，还能自然集成VLM进行语义任务分解，实现复杂的长时域语义引导移动操作行为。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation poses a dual challenge: it requires not only the robust execution of meta-skills but also their seamless, closed-loop chaining equipped with autonomous recovery. Existing approaches remain limited: explicit humanoid-object interaction representations offer precision but are notoriously difficult for high-level planning, whereas implicit skill embeddings are compact but lack the interpretability required for reliable composition. We propose \ours, a hierarchical framework centered on \textbf{contact flow (CF)}, a compact representation consisting of key body trajectories and time-series binary contact signals. Leveraging this shared interface, our low-level policy \textbf{CF-Track} learns a unified library of loco-manipulation skills, while our high-level module \textbf{CF-Gen} heuristically synthesizes future contact-flow sequences. To support this setting, we additionally collect the OmniContact dataset, a MoCap-based HOI corpus for humanoid loco-manipulation (Appendix~\ref{sec:dataset}). Together, they enable robust execution, autonomous failure recovery, and flexible composition of meta-skills for long-horizon tasks. Experiments show that OmniContact achieves \(98.7\%\) success on \textit{Carry Box} and \(76.5\%\) on \textit{Push-Stack Boxes}, outperforming prior baselines by average margins of \(40.9\%\) in meta-skill and \(66.5\%\) in skill chaining. Besides, our framework naturally integrates with VLMs for semantic task decomposition, enabling complex, semantically grounded loco-manipulation behaviors, such as arranging scattered boxes into a heart shape.

</details>


### [267] [RoboTales: ROBOTic Anthropomorphic LEarning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26213)
*Andrew Chen,Ju-Hung Chen,Phurinat Pinyomit,Alexis E. Block*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTales是一个低成本机器人讲故事系统，通过富有表现力的袜子木偶动画化叙事。在Baxter机器人上自主实现，同步旁白、手势和嘴部动作来表演角色驱动的故事。实验表明，木偶式讲故事优于仅手势模式，产生更高的HRIES评分和更好的故事回忆，表明具身木偶表演增强了参与度和叙事理解。系统模块化、平台无关，可适配其他机械臂，为被动媒体提供无屏幕替代方案，支持未来在儿童学习环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种低成本的机器人讲故事系统，通过具身木偶表演增强儿童参与度和叙事理解，提供被动媒体（如屏幕）的无屏幕替代方案。

Method: 在Baxter机器人上自主实现RoboTales系统，同步旁白、手势和嘴部动作；通过木偶表演模式与仅手势模式的对比实验进行评估。

Result: 木偶式讲故事在HRIES评分和故事回忆方面均优于仅手势模式，表明具身木偶表演显著增强参与度和叙事理解。

Conclusion: RoboTales是一种有效且低成本的机器人讲故事方法，可适配其他机械臂，为儿童学习环境提供无屏幕的互动叙事替代方案。

Abstract: RoboTales is a low-cost robotic storytelling system that animates narratives using expressive sock puppetry. Implemented autonomously on a Baxter robot as a test case, RoboTales synchronizes narration, gestures, and mouth movements to perform character-driven stories. In a pilot study, puppet-based storytelling outperformed a gesture-only mode, producing higher HRIES ratings and improved story recall, suggesting that embodied puppetry enhances engagement and narrative comprehension. Designed to be modular and platform-agnostic, RoboTales can be adapted to other manipulators and offers a screen-free alternative to passive media, supporting future deployment in child-centered learning environments.

</details>


### [268] [TaskNPoint: How to Teach Your Humanoid to Hit a Backhand in Minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26215)
*Blake Werner,Ilona Demler,Pietro Perona,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TaskNPoint训练协议，通过人类教练提供少量示范和交互窗口定义，让仿人机器人在单GPU上不到一小时学会动态技能（如网球正反手、踢球、抓取），无需逐任务奖励调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态技能的学习不应依赖大量被动观察（如看比赛视频），而应像人类学习一样，由教练指导关键交互窗口，通过有限示范和针对性练习掌握技能。

Method: TaskNPoint协议：人类教练提供离散技能集、每技能一次示范、交互窗口定义和目标；训练中通过随机化目标采样实现零样本泛化到未见目标位置；在物理仿真环境中填充完整动作轨迹并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在Unitree G1仿人机器人上成功学会网球正反手接球、踢球和箱子抓取放置任务，仅需短人类视频示范和单GPU不到一小时训练，无需逐任务奖励调参。

Conclusion: 明确区分教练与学习者角色的训练范式（少量示范+关键窗口定义+随机目标采样）能高效学习动态技能，是仿人机器人技能学习的有效途径。

Abstract: How do we learn to hit a tennis backhand? Not from a thousand hours of tennis tournaments on TV - we work with a coach and practice. We argue this is also the right recipe for teaching dynamic skills to humanoid robots. This follows from a structural property of dynamic skills: the outcome is decided by a short, crucial portion of the trajectory - for a backhand, the ~20cm of racket travel around ball contact. Getting this interaction window right requires coordinating the whole motion, so that control, physics, and morphology act in concert. Learning thus reduces to mastering a handful of distinct actions and, for each, practicing until the window comes out right. To this end, we introduce TaskNPoint, a training protocol which makes the coach-learner division of labor explicit. The human coach contributes four inputs: a discrete set of skills (e.g. different shots), one demonstration per skill, identification of the interaction window, and the goal. Learning in a physically realistic simulation environment fills in each action trajectory and provides robustness to unmodeled events. Crucially, randomized target sampling during training lets a single demonstration generalize zero-shot to unseen goal locations. We test this approach on a Unitree G1 humanoid that hits forehands and backhands against balls thrown by a human, kicks incoming soccer balls, and picks and places boxes from novel locations. We find that learning is successful from short human video demonstrations and under an hour of training on a single GPU, with no per-task reward tuning.

</details>


### [269] [NavIsaacLab: Generating Realistic Crowd via Parallel Robot Learning for Benchmarking Human-aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26265)
*Bingyi Xia,Han Bao,Jingyu Zhu,Hanjing Ye,Yuhan Pang,Guangcheng Chen,Liang Lin,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NavIsaacLab框架，基于物理真实和照片级逼真的仿真，解决行人感知导航研究中场景数据匮乏、传感器信号不完整和行人行为模拟不真实等问题，支持GPU并行仿真和数据驱动的行人行为建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人感知导航研究面临高质量场景数据稀缺的挑战，现有仿真平台多依赖手工规则近似行人行为，且缺乏对真实传感器信号的模拟，通常假设机器人能获取完美观测。

Method: 基于Isaac Lab构建，采用照片级逼真的场景渲染和GPU并行仿真，提供实时准确的3D视觉反馈；通过轨迹扩散模型和对抗运动学习控制器实现数据驱动的、可控的、基于物理的行人行为模拟；集成跨尺度多样化场景。

Result: 实现了实时、照片级逼真的3D视觉反馈，支持可控的基于物理的行人仿真，提供了跨尺度多样化场景的鲁棒基准。

Conclusion: NavIsaacLab为行人感知导航策略的训练和基准测试提供了全面的仿真框架，解决了现有平台在场景多样性、传感器真实性和行人行为逼真度方面的不足。

Abstract: Robot autonomous navigation that accounts for surrounding human activities is crucial for ensuring both safety and natural human-robot interaction in real-world environments shared by humans and robots. Simulation of complex and diverse navigation scenarios serves as the foundation for training reliable robot navigation policies and accurately evaluating the performance of algorithms, offering an efficient alternative to manual supervision of real data. However, current human-aware navigation research faces significant challenges due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality scene data. Existing simulation platforms often rely on handcrafted rules to approximate pedestrian behavior and lack the capability to provide extensive sensor signals, typically assuming perfect observations. To address these limitations, this paper presents NavIsaacLab, a comprehensive framework for benchmarking and training human-aware navigation policies through physics-based and photo-realistic simulations of pedestrians and scenes. Based on Isaac Lab, the proposed framework employs photo-realistic scene rendering capabilities and supports parallel simulation on GPU, delivering real-time and accurate 3D visual feedback to robots. To enhance the realism of human behavior, a data-driven approach is employed that incorporates a trajectory diffusion model and an adversarial motion learning controller, enabling controllable, physics-based pedestrian simulation. Furthermore, the integration of diverse cross-scale scenes provides a robust benchmark for state-of-the-art human-aware navigation methods.

</details>


### [270] [Racing a Wheeled Quadruped: Active Load Transfer Mitigation via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26313)
*Marla Eisman,Brian Lam,Samuel Sonnino,Francesco Borrelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模型预测控制（MPC）和强化学习（RL）的分层控制框架，用于轮式四足机器人在自主竞速中的主动侧倾控制。通过MPC规划器最小化横向载荷转移率（LTR），并结合全身RL策略直接控制16个执行器。实验表明，主动侧倾控制使平均LTR降低44%，最快圈速提升8.7%，峰值侧向加速度提升21.3%至1.98 m/s²，且高速稳定性优于无侧倾基线控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轮式四足机器人自主竞速中，高速过弯时横向载荷转移会导致车辆侧倾失稳，传统方法缺乏有效的主动侧倾控制来提升高速稳定性和赛道表现。

Method: 提出分层控制框架：1) 离线生成最优赛道线；2) 在线MPC规划器基于车辆动力学自行车模型，主动最小化横向载荷转移率（LTR）；3) 低层全身RL策略直接控制16个执行器，利用腿部膝关节产生抗侧倾扭矩实现主动悬架功能。

Result: 在Unitree Go2-W平台上的实测表明：平均LTR降低44%，最快圈速提升8.7%，峰值侧向加速度从约1.63 m/s²提升至1.98 m/s²（提升21.3%），且高速稳定性显著优于无侧倾基线控制器。

Conclusion: 主动侧倾控制框架有效提升了轮式四足机器人的竞速性能和高速稳定性，验证了将MPC与RL结合用于主动悬架控制的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical control framework using model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) for active roll control to manage lateral load transfer during autonomous racing of a wheeled quadruped. The framework integrates offline time-optimal raceline generation, an online MPC planner that actively minimizes the lateral Load Transfer Ratio (LTR), and a low-level, whole-body RL policy deployed directly onto the robot's 16 actuators. The MPC is based on a vehicle dynamics bicycle model of the Unitree Go2-W platform. The robot's leg actuators act as active suspension where knee joints generate anti-roll torque to bank into turns. Physical track experiments demonstrate that active roll control reduces mean LTR by up to 44%, improves the fastest lap time by 8.7%, and boosts peak lateral acceleration capability by 21.3% to 1.98 $m/s^2$, maintaining robust high-speed stability beyond the range of a non-tilting baseline controller. Supplementary code and video can be found at https://github.com/meisman-ucb/go2w-roll-control-mpc

</details>


### [271] [Layered Outer-Loop Control for Disturbance-Robust Multi-Waypoint UAV Arrival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26315)
*Runfeng Ling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层终端控制架构用于无人机多航点位置调节，并在PyBullet、PX4/Gazebo和真实硬件上进行了三阶段评估，以分离基准测试特定优化与核心控制器设计，从而获得更具可迁移性的鲁棒控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机位置控制在仿真中容易表现良好，但在实际部署中难以兼顾快速逼近、目标附近稳定性和跨场景可靠性。单一基准测试的成功无法保证控制器在更真实闭环环境下的表现，需要一种能够分离核心设计与基准特定优化的评估方法。

Method: 提出分层终端控制架构：Phase I在PyBullet中加随机风扰动进行快速结构选型，分离了平滑逼近生成、持续偏差补偿和有监督近目标终端调节三个模块；Phase II将核心架构迁移至PX4/Gazebo级联飞行栈闭环，暴露延迟敏感和悬停-过渡耦合问题；最后在Vicon跟踪的Tello硬件上完成两级验证。

Result: Phase I中裸控制器达到0.024 m的平均末期风误差；Phase II通过迁移导向规则（无基准先验偏好、跨场景平衡、可部署监督逻辑）选出的控制器在更苛刻闭环评估中保持稳健；Grace补充分析表明部分残差失败集对完成语义敏感而非航点丢失。

Conclusion: 将核心控制器设计与基准特定优化分离，能使基准测试的成功信息量更大，并且在更困难的闭环评估下仍然可辩护。

Abstract: Disturbance-robust UAV position control is easy to demonstrate in benign simulations but much harder to make fast in approach, well behaved near the target, and credible beyond a single benchmark. This letter presents a layered terminal-control architecture for multi-waypoint UAV position regulation together with a staged evaluation across PyBullet, PX4/Gazebo, and hardware. Phase I uses a PyBullet benchmark with stochastic wind for rapid structural selection, identifying a controller core that separates smooth approach generation, persistent-bias compensation, and supervised near-target terminal regulation. Phase II carries only that main architecture into a more demanding PX4/Gazebo closed loop, where the outer-loop controller acts through a cascaded flight stack with delay-sensitive settling and stronger transit-to-hover coupling. This step exposes which benchmark gains survive autopilot-mediated dynamics and which refinements collapse once the loop becomes more deployment-like. In Phase I, the bare controller attains 0.024 m mean late-stage wind error. In Phase II, the final controller is selected using a transfer-oriented rule emphasizing absence of benchmark priors, cross-scenario balance, and deployable supervisory logic. Strict is used as the primary reporting reference; the supplementary retrospective Grace analysis shows that part of the residual failure set is sensitive to completion semantics rather than gross waypoint-miss behaviour. The evaluation is completed on one Vicon-tracked Tello stack through a two-level hardware study. Taken together, the results suggest that benchmark success becomes more informative when the main controller design is separated from benchmark-specific refinement and remains defensible under harder closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [272] [KRVF: A Source-Aware Semantic Voxel World Representation for Edge Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26321)
*Runfeng Ling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KRVF是一种面向边缘移动操作的源感知语义体素世界表征，它将局部世界状态编码为任务导向的体素，包含占用、颜色、语义证据、时间新鲜度和证据来源，并支持深度故障感知的对象推理和映射-感知闭环反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人需要当前、可查询、语义有意义且在边缘计算约束下可用的世界模型。现有以重建为中心的建图管线主要优化全局几何保真度，缺乏对任务导向和源感知的支持。

Method: 提出KRVF表征，将局部世界状态编码为任务导向体素，包含占用、颜色、语义证据、时间新鲜度和证据来源；分离测量占用与语义先验假设；通过渲染地图先验深度实现映射与感知的反馈闭环；暴露任务级查询算子。

Result: 在ROS 2中实现了一个将在线RGB-D观测转化为任务导向机器人记忆的系统，实现了深度故障感知的对象推理且不污染持久几何。

Conclusion: KRVF为边缘移动操作提供了一种源感知、任务导向的语义体素世界表征，通过分离测量与先验、闭环反馈等机制提升了在边缘计算约束下的可用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators need world models that are current, queryable, semantically meaningful, and usable under edge-compute constraints. This technical report presents KRVF, a source-aware semantic voxel world representation for edge mobile manipulation. Unlike reconstruction-centric mapping pipelines that primarily optimize global geometric fidelity, KRVF represents local world state as task-oriented voxels that encode occupancy, color, semantic evidence, temporal freshness, and evidence source. The representation separates measured occupancy from semantic-prior hypotheses, enabling depth-failure-aware object reasoning without silently corrupting persistent geometry. KRVF also closes a feedback loop between mapping and sensing by rendering map-prior depth for repair, and exposes task-level query operators for semantic objects and grasp candidates. The report formalizes the KRVF representation and documents a ROS 2 implementation that turns online RGB-D observations into a task-facing robot memory.

</details>


### [273] [Scaling Nonlinear Optimization: Many Problems One GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26341)
*John Viljoen,Johanna Haffner,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 jaxipm，首个基于 JAX 实现的 GPU 批处理 NLP 求解器，通过异构迭代融合和迭代级批处理技术，在四旋翼非线性模型预测控制基准上实现了相比 IPOPT 最高 32.85 倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成熟 NLP 求解器（如 IPOPT）虽能提供硬约束满足和最优性保证，但受限于 CPU 且每次只能求解单个问题，无法集成到 GPU 批处理学习流水线中。而基于采样的方法得益于 GPU 批处理模拟器，可并行生成大量动力学推演。因此需要一种 GPU 批处理的 NLP 求解器，以解锁在单位时间内生成大量约束局部最优解的相似加速比。

Method: 基于 IPOPT 算法，在 JAX 中实现 GPU 批处理 NLP 求解器。关键技术创新包括：(1) 异构迭代融合 (heterogeneous iteration fusion)——消除控制流；(2) 迭代级批处理 (iteration level batching)——最小化 GPU 空闲时间。

Result: 在四旋翼非线性模型预测控制多个基准任务（障碍物参考跟踪、多四旋翼无碰撞导航、杂乱环境导航）上，相比 IPOPT 实现了最高 32.85 倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: jaxipm 作为首个 GPU 批处理 NLP 求解器，成功弥合了传统 NLP 求解器与 GPU 批处理机器人框架之间的差距，为将 NLP 求解器集成到现代机器人学习流水线中提供了关键基础设施。

Abstract: Many robotics problems, including trajectory optimization, inverse kinematics, and contact-rich motion planning, reduce to nonlinear programs (NLPs). Mature NLP solvers such as IPOPT can solve these problems, offering hard constraint satisfaction, optimality guarantees, and favorable scaling with problem dimension. These solvers underpin gradient-based methods in robotics, yet remain CPU-bound and solve only one problem at a time, preventing their integration into GPU-batched learning pipelines. On the other hand, sampling-based approaches such as reinforcement learning, model predictive path integral, and imitation learning have become the core of modern robotics research due to their ability to leverage GPU-batched simulators. These simulators can generate orders of magnitude more dynamics rollouts per second than was previously possible. If a GPU-batched NLP solver existed, it would unlock similar speedups in the number of constrained, locally optimal solutions generated per second. This regime of solving many problems concurrently versus solving a single problem at a time is a key requirement for integrating NLP solvers in modern GPU-batched robotics frameworks. To this end, we introduce \texttt{jaxipm}, the first GPU-batched NLP solver, based on IPOPT, and implemented in JAX. We accomplish this by redesigning IPOPT's algorithm to eliminate control flow with \textit{heterogeneous iteration fusion}, and by minimizing GPU idle time with \textit{iteration level batching}. We evaluate \texttt{jaxipm} on a variety of quadrotor nonlinear model predictive control benchmarks, including reference tracking in the presence of obstacles, multi-quadrotor navigation without collision, and navigation in a cluttered environment. We demonstrate up to a $32.85\times$ increase in throughput over IPOPT. Our complete open-source codebase is available at https://github.com/johnviljoen/jaxipm.

</details>


### [274] [MPC-Injection: Biasing Off-Policy Locomotion RL Toward Controller-Induced Behavior Basins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26392)
*Roy Xing,Seyoung Ree,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MPC-Injection方法，通过将模型预测控制器生成的转移数据注入强化学习的回放缓冲区，引导策略学习设计者偏好的步态，无需重新设计奖励函数或添加对抗鉴别器，在2D模拟器和Go2四足机器人上实现了与精心调参的奖励塑形和对抗运动先验相当的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在运动控制中常收敛到局部最优但不可部署的行为（如肢体震颤、躯体滑动），无法产生可用步态，需要一种轻量级方法引导RL朝向设计者偏好的步态。

Method: 将模型预测控制（MPC）求解同一MDP时生成的转移数据注入强化学习的回放缓冲区，通过回放状态分布将控制器的偏好行为传递给策略，无需奖励重塑、鉴别器、运动学重定向或辅助目标。

Result: 在2D模拟器行走和Go2四足机器人的sim-to-real评估中，MPC-Injection仅用1-2项任务奖励即可驱动策略进入控制器的行为盆地，产生的步态质量与使用21项调参奖励的奖励塑形以及无鉴别器和重定向开销的对抗运动先验相当。

Conclusion: 注入的转移通过偏置Actor-Critic更新朝向控制器访问过的状态，使策略能够学到纯RL在简单奖励函数下可能无法达到的行为，提供了一种低开销的RL步态引导方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for locomotion frequently converges to locally optimal but undeployable behaviors, such as vibrating limbs or scooting on the torso, that maximize return without producing a usable gait. We present MPC-Injection, a low-overhead method that steers RL toward a designer-preferred gait by inserting transitions into the replay buffer from a model predictive controller solving the same Markov decision process. Unlike reward shaping, MPC-Injection does not require redesigning the task reward, and unlike adversarial imitation learning, it adds no discriminator, no kinematic retargeting, and no auxiliary objective. Instead, the controller's preferred behavior is transferred to the policy purely through the replay state distribution. On a 2D walker in simulation and with sim-to-real evaluation on a Go2 quadruped, we show that MPC-Injection drives the policy into the controller's behavior basin using a one to two-term task reward, producing gaits qualitatively comparable to those of reward shaping with twenty-one tuned terms and of adversarial motion priors without their discriminator and retargeting overhead. We further analyze how the injected transitions bias actor-critic updates toward controller-visited states, allowing the policy to learn behaviors that pure RL may fail to reach under simple reward functions.

</details>


### [275] [Exploring the Intrinsic Geometry of Diffusion Models with Constrained Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26408)
*Miguel Angel Rogel Garcia,Phone Thiha Kyaw,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过在约束逆运动学（IK）这一几何可解析的场景中训练条件扩散模型，实证表明扩散模型能够准确学习数据流形的内在维度及几何结构，且潜在空间中的线性插值能保持在约束流形附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明扩散模型能恢复数据流形的几何结构，但相关证据主要来自自然图像数据，其底层几何结构是未知的。作者希望在一个几何结构可解析计算的场景中验证这一发现，从而提供直接的基准真值。

Method: 针对UR5（6自由度）和Franka（7自由度）两种机器人，针对七种约束族训练单个条件扩散模型。每个约束族定义了配置空间流形，其内在维度是解析已知的。通过模型得分函数提取恢复的内在维度，并与解析自由度数进行比较。

Result: 模型得分函数恢复的内在维度与对应约束流形的解析自由度数完全匹配；潜在空间中的线性插值生成解始终接近相应约束流形，表明模型不仅学会了内在维度，还捕获了约束族的几何结构。

Conclusion: 约束逆运动学为研究扩散模型习得的内在几何提供了一个可控的实验环境，验证了扩散模型在几何可解析场景下确实能够准确学习数据流形的几何性质。

Abstract: Recent studies suggest that diffusion models can recover geometric structure in the data manifolds they are trained on, yet the supporting evidence has so far come mostly from natural-image data, where the underlying geometry itself is unknown. We study this question in a setting where the geometry is analytically tractable: constrained inverse kinematics (IK). Each task-space constraint defines a configuration-space manifold with known intrinsic dimension, giving direct ground truth for evaluating the geometry learned by the model. For each of the 6-DoF UR5 and 7-DoF Franka, we train a single conditional diffusion model across seven constraint families, spanning solution manifolds from discrete IK branches to self-motion manifolds. Our empirical results reveal that the intrinsic dimension recovered from the model's score function matches the analytical degrees of freedom of the corresponding constraint manifold across both robots. Moreover, linear interpolation in the latent space leads to generated solutions that remain close to the appropriate constraint manifold, indicating that the learned representation further captures geometric structure of the constraint family beyond intrinsic dimension alone. Constrained IK therefore offers a controlled setting for studying the intrinsic geometry learned by diffusion models.

</details>


### [276] [CoStream: Composing Simple Behaviors for Generalizable Complex Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26423)
*Haonan Chen,Yuxiang Ma,Stephen Tian,Xiaoshen Han,Wenlong Huang,Feiyang Wu,Yunzhu Li,Jiajun Wu,Edward H. Adelson,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSTREAM框架，通过组合基础模型和多模态感知的三种核心行为（语义、预测、反应），在共享SE(3)接口上实现复杂操作任务的高精度与泛化能力，在8个真实任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在长时域、高精度接触丰富的复杂操作任务中难以同时满足高精度和泛化性：经典方法使用脆弱的任务特定接口实现高精度但难以适应新任务，端到端策略泛化性好但缺乏高精度且需重新训练。

Method: 提出CoSTREAM框架，将基础模型与多模态感知组合为三种可组合的核心行为：语义行为（通过基础模型提取空间约束）、预测行为（通过跟踪关键点在想象视频中预测轨迹）、反应行为（提供高频触觉和力反馈修正）。三者通过右乘在共享SE(3)接口上组合成单一姿态指令，由柔顺控制器执行。

Result: 在8个真实世界任务上验证，涵盖日常操作和精密装配，在接触密集的装配和物体转移任务上提升最为显著，并能从人为扰动中稳健恢复。

Conclusion: 复杂操作能力可以通过简单独立行为的自然组合涌现，无需刚性流水线或端到端整体策略。

Abstract: Long-horizon, contact-rich complex manipulation tasks, such as seating a GPU into a PCIe slot, demand both millimeter high precision and out-of-the-box generalization to new tasks. Existing paradigms struggle to satisfy both: classical pipelines use brittle, task-specific interfaces to achieve high-precision control but require costly pipeline redesigns to adapt to new tasks, whereas monolithic end-to-end policies provide better generalization but lack high precision on complex, out-of-distribution tasks unless retrained with new data. Both paradigms share an implicit assumption: once a manipulation capability is acquired, it must be deployed as a rigid pipeline or monolithic whole, rather than being freely decomposed and recomposed. In this paper, we show that complex manipulation capabilities can emerge naturally from the composition of simple, independent behaviors. Rather than deploying a monolithic policy or a rigid pipeline, we propose \ourshort, a framework orchestrating foundation models and diverse sensing modalities into multiple composable core behaviors: a semantic behavior extracting spatial constraints via foundation models; a predictive behavior forecasting trajectories by tracking keypoints in imagined videos; and a reactive behavior providing high-frequency tactile and force corrections. On a shared $SE(3)$ interface, these outputs compose by right-multiplication into a single pose command at each control step, executed by a compliant controller. We demonstrate \ourshort on 8 real-world tasks spanning everyday manipulation and precision assembly, with the strongest gains in contact-rich assembly and object transfer, and show robust recovery from manual perturbations during execution. {Website:} https://costream-simple.github.io

</details>


### [277] [A System for Fast, Resilient, and Adaptable Loco-Manipulation Behaviors on Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26425)
*Duncan William Calvert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一套面向人形机器人的、可在运行时编辑的本地行为编写与运行系统。该系统结合对象中心化的Affordance模板、行为树逻辑和运行时可编辑的感知机制，通过全身运动控制器实现行走中手臂运动等协调操作。系统在多种人形机器人上部署了20多种真实任务变体，实验表明可在数分钟至数小时内完成对新行为的适配、扩展与组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要在人类建造的物理空间中承担体力消耗大、危险或重复性的工作，但现有系统难以有效协调运动、全身控制、感知、接触和操作员监督等多种能力，缺乏可观测、可预测、可引导的行为编写与执行体系。

Method: 基于Coactive Design原则构建运行时行为编写系统，包含：(1) 与机器人实时同步的操作员界面；(2) 结合Affordance模板、行为树和运行时可编辑感知的行为架构；(3) 支持行走中手臂运动的全身控制器与并发动作分层算法。

Result: 在多种人形机器人（Atlas、Valkyrie、Nadia、H1-2、Alex等）上部署了超过20种真实任务变体，包括推拉不同类型门、多步探索、障碍清除等。实验证明可在数分钟至数小时内完成行为的适配、扩展与组合。

Conclusion: 该系统在能力、速度、可靠性以及行为创建速度方面均表现良好，能够高效地创建新颖的全身移动操作行为，为人形机器人在人类环境中实用化提供了可行的行为编写框架。

Abstract: Humanoid robots could take on physically demanding, hazardous, and repetitive work in spaces built for humans. However, a useful robot for these spaces must coordinate locomotion, whole body motion, perception, contact, and operator supervision. This thesis presents a robot-local, runtime-editable behavior authoring and runtime system.
  Our system strives to be maximally observable, predictable, and directable following Coactive Design principles developed during the DARPA Robotics Challenge. Our operator interface remains continuously synchronized to the robot for runtime authoring, monitoring, and repair. Our behavior architecture uniquely combines object-centric Affordance Templates, organization and logic inspired by Behavior Trees, and runtime-editable perception through a behavior scene and primitive scene actions. Action primitives build on a whole-body controller that supports moving the arms while walking, and use a concurrent action layering algorithm for speed.
  The behavior library developed during this work covers more than twenty real-robot task variants, including push and pull doors with knob, push-bar, and lever-handle mechanisms, multi-step exploration sequences, obstacle clearing, and reactive table-to-table manipulation tasks. This behavior system has been deployed on many humanoid robots, such as Boston Dynamics' DRC Atlas, NASA's Valkyrie, IHMC and Boardwalk Robotics' Nadia, Unitree's H1-2, and IHMC's Alex.
  We evaluate our system across capability, speed, reliability, and speed of behavior creation, adaptation, extension, and combination. Our experiments demonstrate that we can adapt, extend, and combine existing behaviors to create novel loco-manipulation behaviors in minutes or hours. Videos: https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLJK5CTyotYqsfgfnXb-09YNFeBose6uEY.

</details>


### [278] [Play2Perfect: What Matters in Dexterous Play Pretraining for Precise Assembly?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26428)
*Tyler Ga Wei Lum,Kushal Kedia,C. Karen Liu,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Play2Perfect框架，通过任务无关的"玩耍"预训练获取可复用的操作先验（如抓取、手内重定向、位姿到达），再微调适配高精度装配任务，在仅0.5mm间隙的紧装配任务上实现60%成功率，相比从头训练提升33倍样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人难以完成接触丰富、奖励稀疏的精确装配任务，传统模仿学习数据收集困难，强化学习直接探索不可行。作者认为机器人应先学会"玩耍"来获取通用操作能力，再迁移到精密装配。

Method: 提出Play2Perfect框架，分为两个阶段：1) 任务无关的玩耍预训练阶段，在多样物体和目标上进行RL探索，学习可复用的操作先验（抓取、手内重定向、位姿到达）；2) 微调阶段，将预训练先验适配到具体装配任务，聚焦于最终接触丰富的高精度交互。

Result: 预训练先验相比从头训练的RL提升33倍样本效率；零样本sim-to-real迁移实现0.5mm间隙紧装配60%成功率，长时序多部件装配和拧螺丝任务超50%成功率。

Conclusion: 系统研究了玩耍预训练中的关键设计选择（物体多样性、训练目标、轨迹多样性、目标精度），证明了玩耍式预训练可有效解决精密装配中的样本效率和迁移难题。

Abstract: Multi-fingered robots promise the speed and dexterity of human hands, yet challenging problems such as precise assembly have remained out of reach. These tasks are contact-rich, making data collection for imitation learning difficult, and sparse-reward, making direct exploration with reinforcement learning (RL) intractable. Consequently, prior work has made progress by structuring the problem with specialized grippers, tool attachments, and environment fixtures. In this work, we argue that before a robot can perfect precise assembly, it must first learn to play. We further ask the question: what factors in the process of learning to play matter for precise assembly? We propose Play2Perfect, an RL framework for task-agnostic pretraining through play on diverse objects and goals, which is then perfected on precise assembly. The goal of play is to acquire reusable manipulation priors, such as grasping, in-hand reorientation and pose reaching. Finetuning then adapts this general prior to assembly, focusing exploration on the final contact-rich, high-precision interactions needed for success. We systematically study key design choices in play pretraining, including object diversity, training objective, trajectory diversity, and goal precision. We show that our prior is 33x more sample-efficient than RL training from scratch, even when provided with dense, multi-stage rewards. We demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, achieving 60% success on tight insertions with only 0.5 mm contact clearance, and over 50% success on long-horizon multi-part assembly and screwing.

</details>


### [279] [WatchAct: A Benchmark for Behavior-Grounded Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26443)
*Baiqi Li,Ce Zhang,Yu Fang,Yue Yang,Shangzhe Li,Mingyu Ding,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WatchAct是一个基于观察人类行为的机器人操作基准，包含3,000个长时域实例，覆盖事件解析、程序推理、隐式意图推断和情景推理四个能力域。通过解耦评估协议，发现当前最强系统（Gemini-3.1-Pro + π₀.₅）在仿真中仅达16.3%成功率，真实机器人上仅14.0%，远低于人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准仅将指令与单张当前图像配对，无法评估机器人对观察到的连续人类行为（如空间布局、物体历史、手势等）进行推理的能力，缺乏基于视频层面理解人类行为的评估方法。

Method: 构建3,000个长时域实例，每个实例包含真实人类操作视频、语言指令、对齐的仿真场景和可执行的LIBERO任务。设计解耦评估协议，分别测量：(i) 视频到规划的推理能力，(ii) 在oracle规划下的策略执行能力，(iii) 集成规划-策略管线的完整任务完成能力。涵盖事件解析、程序推理、隐式意图推断、情景推理四个认知域。

Result: Gemini-3.1-Pro + π₀.₅管线在仿真中仅16.3%成功率，真实机器人14.0%；Gemini-3.1-Pro规划成功率仅36.8%（人类97.1%）；π₀.₅在oracle规划下任务成功率仅21.5%，域外场景降至10.6%。

Conclusion: 当前系统远未解决基于观察人类行为的机器人操作推理问题，WatchAct揭示了视频理解到任务执行的巨大鸿沟，为未来研究提供了可扩展、可复现的评估框架。

Abstract: A robot working alongside people must reason about what they have done, in what order, and with what intent. Video carries the spatial layouts, object histories, and gestures that language leaves underspecified, yet today's manipulation benchmarks pair an instruction with a single current image, offering no way to evaluate reasoning over observed human behavior. We introduce WatchAct, a benchmark for robot manipulation grounded in observed human behavior. Each instance pairs a real-world human-action video and a language instruction with an aligned simulator scene and an executable LIBERO task, enabling scalable and reproducible evaluation. WatchAct comprises 3,000 long-horizon instances across 14 tasks in four capability domains drawn from the cognitive demands of watching another agent: parsing events (Event Grounding), recovering procedural structure (Procedural Reasoning), inferring unstated intent (Implicit Intent Inference), and tracking how the scene was changed (Episodic Reasoning). We further propose a disentangled evaluation protocol that separately measures (i)~video-to-plan reasoning by vision-language models, (ii)~policy execution under oracle plans, and (iii)~full task completion by integrated planner--policy pipelines. In both simulation and on a Franka Research 3 robot, current systems remain far from solving WatchAct. The best pipeline, Gemini-3.1-Pro with $π_{0.5}$, reaches only 16.3% Success Rate (SR) in simulation and 14.0% on the real robot. Gemini-3.1-Pro attains just 36.8% Plan SR (vs. 97.1% for humans), while $π_{0.5}$ reaches only 21.5% Task SR under oracle plans and drops to 10.6% on out-of-domain scenarios. Dataset and code are available at https://baiqi-li.github.io/watchact_page/.

</details>


### [280] [OSC2Runner: OpenSCENARIO 2.x Compliant High-Fidelity AV Simulation in CARLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26533)
*Thoshitha Gamage,Lasanthi Gamage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OSC2Runner 是首个能将 OpenSCENARIO v2.x DSL 原生映射到 CARLA 仿真器的编排框架，通过多遍 transpiler 架构将场景翻译为确定性行为树，实现 tick 级确定性、精确空间触发评估和 100ms 跨角色黑板同步，将基于场景的测试从近似行为解释转变为数学上严格的执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续仿真框架缺乏对已成熟的 OpenSCENARIO v2.x DSL 的原生执行支持，而基于遗留 ASAM OpenSCENARIO 1.x XML 标准的适配解释器会引入时空漂移、异步事件延迟和人工运动突跳，导致场景测试不精确。

Method: 提出 OSC2Runner 框架，将场景翻译形式化为编译管线，通过多遍 transpiler 架构将类型安全的抽象语法树直接合成为动态确定性行为树（py_trees），并原生映射到 CARLA 的原子 API，绕过静态轨迹回放。

Result: 在高度并发的对抗性案例研究中验证了 tick 级确定性、精确的空间触发评估、100.0 ms 跨角色黑板同步，运动学分析证明严格遵循连续环境边界。

Conclusion: 该架构将基于场景的测试从近似行为解释转变为数学上严格的执行，为协同仿真、硬件在环测试和自动化 LLM 驱动生成管线奠定了确定性后端基础。

Abstract: Scenario-Based Testing predominantly relies on the legacy ASAM OpenSCENARIO 1.x XML standard because existing continuous simulation frameworks lack native execution support for the recently matured v2.x Domain-Specific Language (DSL). Adapting legacy interpreters to evaluate v2.x logic introduces spatiotemporal drift, asynchronous event latencies, and artificial kinematic snapping. Addressing this execution gap, OSC2Runner introduces the first orchestration framework capable of natively mapping the OpenSCENARIO v2.x DSL to CARLA. The framework achieves this by formalizing scenario translation as a compilation pipeline through a multi-pass transpiler architecture. Bypassing static trajectory playback, the architecture synthesizes type-safe Abstract Syntax Trees directly into dynamic deterministic behavior trees (py_trees) natively mapped to CARLA's atomic APIs. Empirical validation in highly concurrent adversarial case studies demonstrates tick-by-tick determinism, exact spatial trigger evaluation, and 100.0 ms cross-actor blackboard synchronization. Kinematic analysis proves the strict adherence to continuous environmental boundaries. This architecture transitions Scenario-Based Testing from approximate behavioral interpretation to mathematically rigorous execution, establishing the deterministic backend required for co-simulation, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and automated LLM-driven generation pipelines.

</details>


### [281] [IDEA: Insensitive to Dynamics Mismatch via Effect Alignment for Sim-to-Real Transfer in Multi-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26575)
*Chenlong Liu,Zhuohui Zhang,Xinyan Chen,Zhipeng Wang,Bin Cheng,Bin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于效应对齐（effect alignment）的多智能体控制仿真到现实迁移方法，通过随机环境结构、离散语义动作和动作同步机制，提升对动力学误差的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于学习和规则的多智能体控制方法在仿真到现实迁移中，因依赖精确动力学建模或系统辨识，在低层控制空间学习的策略对动力学误差高度敏感，导致成本高、脆弱性大。

Method: 1) 结合随机环境结构与离散语义动作，通过闭环控制将策略学习提升到语义抽象层面；2) 开发动作同步机制，缓解智能体间动作时序不匹配问题，增强系统时间一致性。

Result: 在四个多智能体导航任务中，该方法相比主流迁移方法显著提升训练效率，在真实场景中取得更高成功率。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过效应对齐实现了对动力学误差的不敏感性，提高了多智能体系统在动力学失配下的鲁棒性和部署稳定性。

Abstract: Complex multi-agent control tasks remain challenging for traditional rule-based and model-based approaches, motivating the adoption of learning-based methods. However, learning-based methods often struggle with sim-to-real transfer because they rely on accurate dynamics modeling or system identification and learn policies in low-level control spaces that are highly sensitive to dynamics mismatch, making them costly and fragile in complex environments. To address this issue, we propose a sim-to-real method for multi-agent control, which is insensitive to dynamics mismatch via effect alignment. Our method combines random environmental structure with discrete semantic actions through closed-loop control, elevating policy learning to a semantic abstraction level. Additionally, we develop an action synchronization mechanism that mitigates inter-agent action timing mismatches, thereby enhancing the temporal consistency of the system. Experiments on four multi-agent navigation tasks demonstrate that our method substantially improves training efficiency over mainstream transfer methods and achieves higher success rates in real-world scenarios, thereby improving the robustness and deployment stability of multi-agent systems under dynamics mismatch.

</details>


### [282] [Inference-Time Robot Behavior Steering through Physically-Aware Reconfiguration of Task-Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26588)
*Yiyuan Pan,Hanjiang Hu,Shangtao Li,Xusheng Luo,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReStruct提出了一种基于神经自动机策略的方法，通过在保留底层连续控制器的情况下，利用同步乘积将用户偏好整合到高层状态机骨架中，实现无需重训练的测试时行为引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在测试时行为引导上存在两难：端到端方法需要微调或专家级引导，神经符号方法依赖预定义符号导致编辑结果可能逻辑合理但物理不可行。

Method: 基于神经自动机策略，将视觉运动策略分解为高层状态机骨架（捕获任务结构）和低层残差连续控制器。通过同步乘积将偏好整合到骨架中，在保持控制器冻结的情况下更新动作先验，实现物理感知控制。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，ReStruct在任务成功率和偏好遵循度上均超越现有方法，包括比VLA模型高出最多25%。

Conclusion: ReStruct能够有效引导从目标规范到时序逻辑约束的广泛偏好，在不重训练的情况下实现物理可行的行为重配置。

Abstract: A central challenge in deploying learned robot policies is inference-time behavior steering: redirecting a policy at test time to satisfy user preferences not anticipated during training, without retraining. Existing methods fail in two modes: end-to-end methods require fine-tuning or expert-level guidance, while neuro-symbolic methods rely on predefined symbols whose edits can result in logically reasonable but physically infeasible plans. To address this challenge, we propose ReStruct, which builds upon a neural automaton policy that decomposes a visuomotor policy into a high-level state-machine skeleton capturing task structure and a low-level continuous controller represented as a residual policy. Specifically, ReStruct adopts the automaton to represent the preference and incorporates it into the skeleton through a synchronous product, thereby reconfiguring the task structure. With the controller kept frozen, the action priors provided by the skeleton are updated accordingly to enable physically-aware control under a modified task structure. Extensive experiments from simulation and real-world show that ReStruct steers a wide range of preferences, from object-centric specifications to temporal-logic constraints, and after steering surpasses existing methods, exceeding VLA models in both task success and preference-following by up to 25%.

</details>


### [283] [Bridging Handheld and Teleoperated Supervision for Contact-Rich Manipulation via State-Gated Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26603)
*Vidullan Surendran,Neehar Peri,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BRIDGE方法，通过混合手持采集数据与少量遥操作数据，并利用混合专家模型按任务阶段路由，解决了接触丰富操控任务中数据效率与安全性的平衡问题，成功率提升最高36.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持数据采集系统（如UMI）可扩展但只能采集观察到的动作而非期望动作；遥操作能采集期望动作但耗时。现有方法无法平衡两种数据源在接触丰富操控任务中的互补优势。

Method: 提出BRIDGE（双模态模仿数据门控专家路由），采用混合扩散策略专家模型，根据当前机器人状态在任务阶段特定头之间路由，使接触敏感阶段能使用期望动作进行监督。

Result: 在三种接触丰富操控任务上，相比仅使用手持数据基线，成功率提升最高36.7%。

Conclusion: 混合手持数据与少量针对性遥操作数据是一种高效的混合策略；简单混合会导致性能下降，而BRIDGE通过阶段感知路由有效解决了数据分布不匹配问题。

Abstract: Handheld data collection systems, such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), enable scalable data collection across diverse environments but only capture observed actions rather than the desired actions executed by a robot controller. In contrast, teleoperation captures desired actions directly, but is prohibitively time-consuming to collect. We revisit this trade-off through the lens of action validity across task phases. We observe that handheld trajectories provide valid supervision in tolerant, free-space phases, but lack dynamic feasibility in contact-sensitive phases, where tracking observed trajectories at high stiffness produces large, unsafe contact forces. We study the interaction between these two supervision types for contact-rich manipulation and find that training policies that combine handheld data with a small number of targeted teleoperated demonstrations provide an efficient hybrid strategy. Specifically, rather than teleoperating the entire task, we only collect partial teleoperated demonstrations for task segments where base handheld policies fail. However, naively mixing handheld and teleoperated phase-specific data yields worse performance than training on handheld data alone. To address this mismatch between observed and desired supervision, we propose Bi-modal Routing for Imitation Data via Gated Experts (BRIDGE), a mixture of diffusion policy experts that routes between specialist task phase heads conditioned on the current robot state. Notably, our approach enables task-phase specific use of desired actions during contact sensitive segments and improves success rates over handheld-only baselines by up to 36.7% across three contact-rich manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [284] [A Closed-Form 4-DoF Inter-Robot Pose Estimator using Bearing-only Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26616)
*Qixin De,Ao Zhuang,Yechen Zhang,Zhuozhou Qian,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于测程-方位角的协同定位方法，通过封闭式4自由度位姿估计器、可观测性分析与自主观测测试模块，在降低计算成本的同时提高了6自由度协同定位的精度与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6自由度协同定位方法在特定运动模式下存在可观测性退化问题，难以快速获得准确且可靠的机器人间位姿估计。

Method: 提出封闭式4自由度位姿估计器（松弛旋转非线性约束+误差投影平移估计）；进行可观测性理论分析；引入可观测性测试模块以自主确定最优估计时刻。

Result: 所提算法在显著降低计算开销的同时实现更高估计精度；可观测性测试模块在保证估计可靠性的同时最小化数据采集间隔。

Conclusion: 4自由度系统对运动激励要求更宽松，在更广泛的协同机动下仍能实现可靠估计，且算法整体具备低计算成本与高精度优势。

Abstract: Bearing-odometry-based cooperative localization has attracted increasing research interest due to its minimal infrastructure requirements, low communication bandwidth and broad applicability in complex environments. However, existing 6-DoF approaches still face challenges in rapidly obtaining accurate and reliable inter-robot pose estimation, as the system is prone to observability degeneracy under specific motion patterns. To address these issues, we first propose a closed-form 4-DoF inter-robot pose estimator, which relaxes nonlinear constraints for rotations estimation and employs error projection for translations estimation. We then conduct a theoretical analysis of the system's observability, identifying degeneracy under two typical motion patterns: collinear and shape-preserving formations. The analysis further shows that the proposed 4-DoF system requires less stringent motion excitation for observability, enabling reliable estimation under a broader range of cooperative maneuvers. Furthermore, an observability test module is introduced to autonomously determine the optimal estimation instant, eliminating reliance on a predefined fixed-length sliding window. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieves higher estimation accuracy with significantly low computational cost, and the observability test module ensures estimation reliability while minimizing the data collection interval.

</details>


### [285] [Hardware Design for Table Tennis Robot Capable of Beating Professional Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26643)
*Nobuhiko Mukai,Pavel Adodin,Stefan Heusser,Alexander Sigrist,Divij Grover,Guillem Torrente,Farshad Khadivar,Takekazu Kakinuma,Peter Dürr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了击败职业选手的乒乓球机器人硬件规格，开发了8自由度机器人"Ace"，通过拓扑优化、逆动力学模型和强化学习控制，实现了0.8秒全挥杆周期、22米/秒峰值拍速，并成功击败多名职业选手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏能够真正击败职业乒乓球选手的机器人硬件系统，需要明确击败职业选手所需的硬件规格指标，并据此设计制造高性能机器人。

Method: 首先分析精英选手运动，定义工作空间、负载、外力抵抗、物理性能、发球能力和末端执行器精度等目标规格；通过拓扑优化最小化质量同时保持刚度；利用逆动力学扭矩模型优化电机和减速器选型；建立带延迟补偿的低阶关节动力学模型并集成到仿真中，使用强化学习控制策略。

Result: 机器人实现了0.8秒的全挥杆周期和22米/秒的峰值拍速，在实际对战中成功击败了多名职业乒乓球选手。

Conclusion: 通过系统化的硬件规格分析、结构优化和动力学建模，所开发的Ace机器人证明了机器人可以具备击败职业乒乓球运动员的硬件能力，为高性能运动机器人设计提供了参考。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the hardware specifications required for a table tennis robot to beat professional players. After analyzing the motions of elite players, we defined target specifications for the workspace, payload, external-force resistance, physical performance, serve capability, and end-effector accuracy. Based on these specifications, we developed "Ace", a custom 8-DoF robot. The mechanical structure was improved through topology optimization to minimize mass while preserving stiffness. Motor and gearbox selection was optimized using an inverse-dynamics torque model. Low-order per-joint dynamics models with delay compensation were identified and integrated into simulation to enable the use of an RL control policy. Experiments demonstrated repeated full-stroke swings with a cycle time of 0.8 s and a peak racket-center velocity of 22 m/s. The robot successfully defeated multiple professional players.

</details>


### [286] [LAMP: Lane-Aligned Motion Primitives for Feasible Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26661)
*Sangjin Han,Hoseong Jung,Jeongtae Her,Changhyun Choi,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMP框架，通过VQ-VAE学习与车道拓扑对齐的运动基元作为离散意图查询，并引入可行性感知意图选择器过滤不可达意图，在保持预测精度的同时显著提升轨迹可行性与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动预测方法仅关注最小化位移误差，忽略多模态预测对车道拓扑的依从性，导致低概率模式的预测轨迹可能违反物理和逻辑约束，对安全关键规划不可靠。

Method: 使用VQ-VAE学习形状感知的运动基元作为离散意图查询；设计可行性感知意图选择器，利用车道拓扑先验过滤不可达意图查询；引导解码器优先选择拓扑一致的意图，同时保持行为多样性。

Result: 在Argoverse 2数据集上，LAMP取得了与现有最优基线相当的预测精度，并在可行性和多样性指标上超越它们。

Conclusion: 锚定车道拓扑的离散运动基元能够有效提升多模态轨迹预测的约束依从性，为安全关键规划提供更可靠的预测集。

Abstract: Motion forecasting is essential for autonomous driving systems to enable safe decision-making and planning in complex driving scenarios. While existing predictors excel at minimizing standard displacement errors, they often overlook the adherence to lane topology of multimodal predictions, particularly for lower-probability modes. Consequently, predicted trajectories may violate physical and logical constraints, making the prediction set unreliable for safety-critical planning. In this paper, we propose LAMP (Lane-Aligned Motion Primitives), a topology-aware forecasting framework that anchors multimodal prediction to structured motion primitives aligned with lane topology. Specifically, we use a VQ-VAE to learn shape-aware motion primitives as discrete intention queries, capturing spatiotemporal patterns beyond endpoint-based intentions. We further introduce a feasibility-aware intention selector trained with a lane-topology prior for filtering unreachable intention queries, guiding the decoder to prioritize topology-consistent intentions while preserving behavioral diversity. Extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 dataset demonstrate that LAMP achieves prediction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art baselines while outperforming them in feasibility and diversity metrics.

</details>


### [287] [Tactile-WAM: Touch-Aware World Action Model with Tactile Asymmetric Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26663)
*Siyu Wu,Linjing You,Junjie Zhu,Yaozu Liu,Changhao Zhang,Jian Liu,Weiqiang Wang,Qi Li,Jituo Li,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Tactile-WAM，通过触觉非对称注意力机制（TAAM）解决触觉污染问题，在接触密集操作任务中将平均成功率提升38.9%，接触密集任务提升86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触密集操作中，仅依赖视觉预测可能导致物理不完整（如滑动、卡住、接触法线等弱可见信息）。现有方法尝试预测未来触觉状态，但直接注入触觉token会导致"触觉污染"，即未约束的触觉信号迫使视觉动力学模型吸收稀疏、局部的事件驱动信号，从而降低视频和动作预测质量。

Method: 提出Tactile-WAM，核心是触觉非对称注意力机制（TAAM）。TAAM包含两个组件：(1) VideoClean掩码，阻止视频查询访问触觉键/值token，同时保留动作查询的访问权限，保护视觉预测；(2) 触觉感知偏置，基于预测的触觉变化调制动作注意力对触觉token的关注。

Result: 在ManiFeel基准上，Tactile-WAM整体平均成功率提升38.9%，接触密集任务成功率提升86%。

Conclusion: 通过非对称的注意力设计，在保持视觉预测质量的同时有效利用触觉信息指导动作生成，为解决多模态融合中的模态干扰问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) generate actions together with predicted futures, offering a powerful interface for robot decision making. In contact-rich manipulation, however, visually plausible futures can be physically incomplete: insertion, assembly, search, and reorientation often depend on slip, jamming, contact normals, or small alignment errors that are weakly visible or hidden in RGB. A natural solution is to predict future tactile states, however, we identify tactile pollution, a failure mode where unconstrained tactile-token injection degrades video and action prediction by forcing a visual dynamics model to absorb sparse, local, event-driven contact signals. To address this, we propose Tactile-WAM, a touch-aware WAM with a Tactile Asymmetric Attention Mechanism (TAAM). TAAM combines a VideoClean mask, which blocks video-query access to tactile key/value tokens while preserving action-query access, with a touch-aware bias for action attention. The VideoClean mask protects visual prediction while keeping contact information available for action generation; the touch-aware bias is derived from predicted touch changes and modulates action attention to tactile tokens during denoising. On ManiFeel, Tactile-WAM improves the mean success rate by 38.9% overall and by 86% on contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [288] [Learning Motion Feasibility from Point Clouds in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26700)
*Sajid Ansari,Arthi,Girish Varma,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了从原始RGB-D观测中直接学习7自由度机械臂在杂乱场景中的运动可行性预测问题，构建了包含270万抓取可行性标签的大规模基准数据集，并对比了MLP、体积CNN和点云Transformer三类模型，其中点云Transformer模型GRASPFC-PTX在新物体上达到0.996的AUROC，且预测速度显著快于基于采样的运动规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中，基于采样的运动规划器（SBMP）进行不可行规划尝试会消耗大量计算资源，而现有不可行性认证方法局限于低维配置空间且假设简化的几何环境。因此需要直接从原始RGB-D观测中学习运动可行性预测，以加速机器人在真实杂乱场景中的规划过程。

Method: 构建了包含270万抓取可行性标签的大规模基准数据集（88个扫描物体、190个杂乱桌面场景），在匹配训练条件下对比了三类分类器架构：MLP、体积CNN和基于点云的Transformer（GRASPFC-PTX）。

Result: 最佳模型GRASPFC-PTX（点云Transformer）在新物体上达到0.996的AUROC，预测速度显著快于SBMP。

Conclusion: 基于点云Transformer的运动可行性预测方法能够在真实杂乱场景中高效准确地预测运动可行性，在精度上接近完美且速度远超传统基于采样的方法，为机器人任务与运动规划提供了有效替代方案。

Abstract: Motion feasibility prediction plays a central role in robotics, particularly in task and motion planning and manipulation. A major bottleneck for this problem in cluttered environments is that infeasible planning attempts by Sampling-based motion planners (SBMPs) can incur substantial computational cost. Also existing approaches for infeasibility certification are limited to low-dimensional configuration spaces and often assume simplified geometric environments represented by primitive objects with known parameters. We study the complementary problem of learning motion feasibility prediction directly from raw RGB-D observations for a 7-DOF manipulator operating in realistic cluttered scenes. We introduce the first large-scale benchmark for this setting, comprising 2.7M grasp feasibility labels over 88 scanned objects and 190 cluttered tabletop scenes. We benchmark three representative classifier families spanning MLP- based, volumetric-CNN, and point-cloud-based Transformer architectures under matched training conditions. Our best model, GRASPFC-PTX (a point-cloud transformer), achieves an AUROC of 0.996 on Novel objects while providing predictions significantly faster than SBMPs.

</details>


### [289] [PressMimic: Pressure-Guided Motion Capture and Control for Humanoid Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26741)
*Yi Lu,Shenghao Ren,Tianyu Xiong,Zhaoxiang Li,Jiaqi Li,He Zhang,Tao Yu,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO
